"""Public schedule backends only; metadata must never trigger archive login."""

import fastf1

from .loading_errors import SessionDataError


def _public(call, *args, **kwargs):
    failure = None
    for backend in ("fastf1", "ergast"):
        try:
            return call(*args, **kwargs, backend=backend)
        except Exception as exc:
            failure = exc
    raise SessionDataError(
        "schedule_provider_error",
        "The public F1 schedule could not be loaded. Retry shortly.",
        502,
        20,
    ) from failure


def get_session(year, event, kind):
    return _public(fastf1.get_session, year, event, kind)


def get_schedule(year, **kwargs):
    return _public(fastf1.get_event_schedule, year, **kwargs)


def get_event(year, event):
    return _public(fastf1.get_event, year, event)


============================================================
FILE: ./backend/services/results.py
============================================================
"""Published classifications via Jolpica, independent of protected timing streams."""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from functools import lru_cache
import time

import fastf1
from fastf1.ergast import Ergast
import pandas as pd

from .loading_errors import SessionDataError
from . import events


@dataclass
class ResultsSession:
    event: object
    date: object
    name: str
    results: pd.DataFrame
    published_results: bool = True
    data_source: str = "Jolpica-F1 published classification"
    data_warnings: list = field(default_factory=list)


@lru_cache(maxsize=64)
def _load(year, event_key, kind, bucket):
    template = events.get_session(year, event_key, kind)
    now = pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC")
    start = pd.Timestamp(template.date)
    if start.tzinfo is None:
        start = start.tz_localize("UTC")
    if start > now:
        raise SessionDataError(
            "session_not_started", "This session has not started yet.", 425, 60
        )
    client = Ergast()
    method = {
        "Q": client.get_qualifying_results,
        "R": client.get_race_results,
        "S": client.get_sprint_results,
    }.get(kind)
    if method is None:
        raise SessionDataError(
            "classification_not_supported",
            "This session needs raw timing data rather than a public classification.",
            422,
        )
    try:
        response = method(
            season=year, round=int(template.event["RoundNumber"]), limit=100
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        raise SessionDataError(
            "classification_provider_error",
            "Published results could not be loaded from Jolpica. Retry shortly.",
            502,
            20,
        ) from exc
    if not response.content or response.content[0].empty:
        raise SessionDataError(
            "classification_not_published",
            "The session classification has not been published by Jolpica yet.",
            425,
            60,
        )
    frame = (
        response.content[0]
        .copy()
        .rename(
            columns={
                "number": "DriverNumber",
                "driverCode": "Abbreviation",
                "givenName": "FirstName",
                "familyName": "LastName",
                "constructorName": "TeamName",
                "position": "Position",
                "grid": "GridPosition",
                "status": "Status",
                "points": "Points",
                "laps": "Laps",
                "totalRaceTime": "Time",
            }
        )
    )
    required = {"DriverNumber", "Abbreviation", "TeamName", "Position"}
    if not required.issubset(frame.columns):
        raise SessionDataError(
            "classification_incomplete",
            "Published results are missing required driver or position fields.",
            502,
        )
    frame["DriverNumber"] = frame["DriverNumber"].astype(str)
    frame["FullName"] = frame.get("FirstName", "") + " " + frame.get("LastName", "")
    for column in ("Q1", "Q2", "Q3"):
        frame[column] = (
            pd.to_timedelta(frame[column].astype(object), errors="coerce")
            if column in frame
            else pd.Series(pd.NaT, index=frame.index, dtype="timedelta64[ns]")
        )
    frame["Position"] = pd.to_numeric(frame["Position"], errors="coerce")
    frame = frame.dropna(subset=["Position", "Abbreviation"]).sort_values("Position")
    if frame.empty:
        raise SessionDataError(
            "classification_incomplete",
            "No classified driver positions are available.",
            425,
            60,
        )
    if kind == "Q" and not frame[["Q1", "Q2", "Q3"]].notna().any().any():
        raise SessionDataError(
            "qualifying_times_unavailable",
            "Qualifying positions are published but timed laps are not available yet.",
            425,
            60,
        )
    return ResultsSession(
        template.event,
        template.date,
        template.name,
        frame.set_index("DriverNumber", drop=False),
    )


def load_results_session(year, event, kind):
    return _load(year, event, kind, int(time.time() // 300))


============================================================
FILE: ./backend/services/ical.py
============================================================
"""CLI import of a downloaded publisher calendar. No arbitrary server URL fetches."""

import argparse
import json
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlparse

from icalendar import Calendar


def parse_ics(text, series, source_url, verified_at):
    if urlparse(source_url).scheme != "https":
        raise ValueError("Use an HTTPS publisher URL for provenance")
    calendar = Calendar.from_ical(text)
    result = []
    for item in calendar.walk("VEVENT"):
        if item.get("RRULE") or item.get("RECURRENCE-ID"):
            raise ValueError(
                "Recurring calendars must be expanded to individual events before import"
            )
        if str(item.get("STATUS", "")).upper() == "CANCELLED":
            continue
        start = item.decoded("DTSTART")
        end = item.decoded("DTEND", None)
        all_day = isinstance(start, date) and not isinstance(start, datetime)
        if not all_day and (start.tzinfo is None or start.utcoffset() is None):
            raise ValueError(
                "A floating session time has no timezone; add TZID or UTC before importing"
            )
        if all_day:
            end = end or start + timedelta(days=1)
            if isinstance(end, datetime) or end <= start:
                raise ValueError("Invalid all-day end date")
        else:
            if end is not None and (
                not isinstance(end, datetime) or end.tzinfo is None or end <= start
            ):
                raise ValueError("Invalid timed event end")
            start = start.astimezone(timezone.utc)
            end = end.astimezone(timezone.utc) if end else None
        result.append(
            {
                "id": f'{series}:{item.get("UID", start.isoformat())}',
                "series": series,
                "title": str(item.get("SUMMARY", "Session")),
                "event": str(item.get("SUMMARY", "Session")),
                "start": start.isoformat(),
                "end": end.isoformat() if end else None,
                "dateOnly": all_day,
                "location": str(item.get("LOCATION", "")),
                "source": "Imported publisher calendar",
                "sourceUrl": source_url,
                "verifiedAt": verified_at,
                "status": "imported schedule",
            }
        )
    return result


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Merge a downloaded .ics into a motorsport year snapshot"
    )
    parser.add_argument("file", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--series",
        required=True,
        choices=["wec", "imsa", "nls", "gt-europe", "supercars", "n24"],
    )
    parser.add_argument("--source-url", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--year", required=True, type=int)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.file.stat().st_size > 2_000_000:
        parser.error("Calendar exceeds 2 MB")
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    events = parse_ics(args.file.read_bytes(), args.series, args.source_url, now)
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "data" / f"motorsport-{args.year}.json"
    content = (
        json.loads(path.read_text()) if path.exists() else {"events": [], "sources": []}
    )
    # Replace this series/year atomically, including removals in the new publisher feed.
    content["events"] = [e for e in content["events"] if e["series"] != args.series] + [
        e for e in events if int(e["start"][:4]) == args.year
    ]
    content["sources"] = [
        s for s in content["sources"] if s["series"] != args.series
    ] + [{"series": args.series, "url": args.source_url, "verifiedAt": now}]
    path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    temp = path.with_suffix(".tmp")
    temp.write_text(json.dumps(content, indent=2))
    temp.replace(path)
    print(
        f"Updated {path} with {len(events)} parsed entries; inspect the diff before committing"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


============================================================
FILE: ./backend/services/data.py
============================================================
"""One cache configuration and bounded, synchronized FastF1 session loading."""

import json
import os
import threading
import time
import logging
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path

import fastf1
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
DATA_DIR = Path(os.environ.get("F1_DATA_DIR", ROOT / "var"))
DATA_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
from . import f1_access
from . import openf1
from . import events
from .loading_errors import SessionDataError
from .results import load_results_session

CACHE_DIR = f1_access.configure_cache(ROOT, DATA_DIR)
f1_access.install_server_policy()
LOG = logging.getLogger(__name__)
_FAILURES = {}
SESSION_LOCK = threading.RLock()


def utc_now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def iso(value):
    if value is None or pd.isna(value):
        return None
    ts = pd.Timestamp(value)
    if ts.tzinfo is None:
        ts = ts.tz_localize("UTC")
    return ts.tz_convert("UTC").isoformat()


def number(value, digits=4):
    try:
        value = float(value)
        return round(value, digits) if np.isfinite(value) else None
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return None


def seconds(value):
    return number(pd.Timedelta(value).total_seconds()) if pd.notna(value) else None


def optional_data(session, name):
    try:
        return getattr(session, name, None)
    except Exception:
        return None


def _valid(session, telemetry=False):
    laps = optional_data(session, "laps")
    if (
        laps is None
        or laps.empty
        or "LapTime" not in laps
        or not laps["LapTime"].notna().any()
    ):
        return False
    if telemetry:
        for channel in ("car_data", "pos_data"):
            stream = optional_data(session, channel)
            if not stream or not any(
                frame is not None and not frame.empty for frame in stream.values()
            ):
                return False
    return True


def _try_load(session, telemetry):
    try:
        session.load(laps=True, telemetry=telemetry, weather=True, messages=True)
    except SystemExit as exc:
        # FastF1 may call exit() on an unusable parsed cache. Keep the API alive.
        raise SessionDataError(
            "archive_cache_unusable",
            "FastF1 could not refresh an incompatible cached session. Authenticate and retry; existing cache files have been preserved.",
            502,
        ) from exc


@lru_cache(maxsize=8)
def _session(year, event, kind, telemetry, bucket, auth_fingerprint):
    f1_access.check_version()
    session = fastf1.get_session(year, event, kind)
    start = pd.Timestamp(session.date)
    if start.tzinfo is None:
        start = start.tz_localize("UTC")
    if start > pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC"):
        raise SessionDataError(
            "session_not_started", "This session has not started yet.", 425, 60
        )
    # Read existing parsed/HTTP caches without changing any other thread's mode
    # or triggering authentication. Failed/partial objects never enter this LRU.
    if f1_access.has_cached_timing(session, CACHE_DIR):
        try:
            with f1_access.cache_only():
                _try_load(session, telemetry)
            if _valid(session, telemetry):
                return _annotate(session)
        except (Exception, SystemExit):
            pass
    f1_access.ensure_authenticated()
    session = fastf1.get_session(year, event, kind)
    f1_access.probe_archive(session)
    _try_load(session, telemetry)
    if not _valid(session, telemetry):
        raise SessionDataError(
            "timing_data_incomplete",
            "The archive responded but the required lap timing or position/car data did not load. Retry later or run python -m backend.check_data with --timing for diagnostics.",
            502,
            30,
        )
    return _annotate(session)


def _annotate(session):
    session.data_source = "FastF1 timing archive / cache"
    session.data_warnings = []
    for key, label in (
        ("weather_data", "Weather"),
        ("race_control_messages", "Race-control messages"),
    ):
        frame = optional_data(session, key)
        if frame is None or frame.empty:
            session.data_warnings.append(
                f"{label} unavailable; lap timing remains usable. Missing channels are not reconstructed."
            )
    return session


def timing_provider():
    provider = os.environ.get("F1_TIMING_PROVIDER", "auto").strip().lower()
    if provider not in ("auto", "openf1", "fastf1"):
        raise SessionDataError(
            "timing_provider_invalid",
            "F1_TIMING_PROVIDER must be auto, openf1, or fastf1.",
            503,
        )
    return provider


@lru_cache(maxsize=8)
def _public_session(year, event, kind, provider, bucket):
    template = events.get_session(year, event, kind)
    if provider == "auto" and f1_access.has_cached_timing(template, CACHE_DIR):
        try:
            with f1_access.cache_only():
                _try_load(template, False)
            if _valid(template):
                return _annotate(template)
        except (Exception, SystemExit):
            pass
    return openf1.load_session(year, event, kind, DATA_DIR / "openf1", template)


def load_session(year, event, kind, telemetry=False, *, results_only=False):
    if results_only:
        return load_results_session(year, event, kind)
    provider = timing_provider()
    state = f1_access.auth_status()
    # CLI login after an error invalidates the short failure cache immediately.
    token_expiry = None
    if state["authenticated"]:
        token_expiry = f1_access._auth_module().token_manager.get_stored().expires_at
    fingerprint = (
        state["authenticated"],
        state["credentialsConfigured"],
        token_expiry,
        fastf1.__version__,
    )
    key = (year, event, kind, telemetry, fingerprint, provider)
    with SESSION_LOCK:
        failed = _FAILURES.get(key)
        if failed and failed[0] > time.monotonic():
            raise failed[1]
        try:
            bucket = int(time.time() // 300)
            if provider == "fastf1":
                result = _session(year, event, kind, telemetry, bucket, fingerprint)
            else:
                try:
                    result = _public_session(year, event, kind, provider, bucket)
                    # Cached native sessions might lack the requested telemetry.
                    if (
                        telemetry
                        and getattr(result, "data_provider", None) != "openf1"
                        and not _valid(result, True)
                    ):
                        result = openf1.load_session(
                            year, event, kind, DATA_DIR / "openf1"
                        )
                except SessionDataError:
                    if provider != "auto" or not (
                        state["authenticated"] or state["credentialsConfigured"]
                    ):
                        raise
                    result = _session(year, event, kind, telemetry, bucket, fingerprint)
            _FAILURES.pop(key, None)
            return result
        except SessionDataError as exc:
            if len(_FAILURES) > 64:
                _FAILURES.clear()
            _FAILURES[key] = (time.monotonic() + 15, exc)
            raise
        except Exception as exc:
            LOG.warning("Session timing load failed (%s)", type(exc).__name__)
            error = SessionDataError(
                "timing_load_failed",
                "The timing provider could not load this session. Run python -m backend.check_data with --timing for diagnostics.",
                502,
                15,
            )
            _FAILURES[key] = (time.monotonic() + 15, error)
            raise error from exc


def finished(session):
    if getattr(session, "data_provider", None) == "openf1":
        return session.completed
    if getattr(session, "published_results", False):
        return True
    status = optional_data(session, "session_status")
    return (
        status is not None
        and not status.empty
        and status["Status"].astype(str).iloc[-1] in ("Finished", "Finalised")
    )


def clean_laps(laps):
    """Conservative green-flag sample. Traffic is NOT inferred from lap time."""
    df = laps.copy()
    if df.empty:
        return df
    if "OpenF1Eligible" in df.columns:
        df = df.loc[df["OpenF1Eligible"].eq(True)].copy()
        df["seconds"] = df["LapTime"].dt.total_seconds()
        return df.drop_duplicates(["Driver", "LapNumber"])
    required = {"LapTime", "IsAccurate", "TrackStatus", "PitInTime", "PitOutTime"}
    if not required.issubset(df.columns):
        return df.iloc[:0]
    mask = (
        df["LapTime"].notna()
        & (df["LapTime"].dt.total_seconds() > 0)
        & df["IsAccurate"].eq(True)
        & df["TrackStatus"].astype(str).eq("1")
        & df["PitInTime"].isna()
        & df["PitOutTime"].isna()
    )
    for column in ("Deleted", "FastF1Generated"):
        if column in df:
            mask &= ~df[column].fillna(False).astype(bool)
    df = df.loc[mask].copy()
    df["seconds"] = df["LapTime"].dt.total_seconds()
    return df.drop_duplicates(["Driver", "LapNumber"])


def json_safe(value):
    """Reject NaN/Infinity before caching or returning calculated payloads."""
    return json.loads(json.dumps(value, allow_nan=False))


============================================================
FILE: ./backend/services/loading_errors.py
============================================================
"""Typed provider failures; never disguise upstream access errors as missing routes."""

from fastapi import HTTPException


class SessionDataError(HTTPException):
    def __init__(self, code, message, status_code=503, retry_after=None):
        self.code = code
        headers = {"X-F1-Data-Error": code}
        if retry_after is not None:
            headers["Retry-After"] = str(retry_after)
        super().__init__(status_code=status_code, detail=message, headers=headers)


============================================================
FILE: ./backend/services/openf1_client.py
============================================================
"""Public historical OpenF1 transport with a shared disk cache and rate limit.

No account, token, browser, or F1TV authentication is used here. SQLite shares
the 30/minute budget between API workers using the same F1_DATA_DIR.
"""

import hashlib
import json
import sqlite3
import threading
import time
from contextlib import contextmanager
from email.utils import parsedate_to_datetime
from pathlib import Path

import requests

from .loading_errors import SessionDataError

BASE_URL = "https://api.openf1.org/v1"
ENDPOINTS = {
    "sessions",
    "drivers",
    "laps",
    "stints",
    "pit",
    "race_control",
    "weather",
    "session_result",
    "car_data",
    "location",
}


class OpenF1Client:
    def __init__(self, cache_dir):
        self.path = Path(cache_dir) / "openf1.sqlite3"
        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.lock = threading.RLock()
        with self.connect() as db:
            db.execute(
                "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS responses "
                "(key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, expires REAL NOT NULL, body TEXT NOT NULL)"
            )
            db.execute(
                "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS budget "
                "(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1), next_request REAL NOT NULL)"
            )
            db.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO budget VALUES (1, 0)")

    @contextmanager
    def connect(self):
        db = sqlite3.connect(self.path, timeout=15)
        try:
            with db:
                yield db
        finally:
            db.close()

    def _reserve(self):
        with self.connect() as db:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            now = time.time()
            next_request = db.execute(
                "SELECT next_request FROM budget WHERE id=1"
            ).fetchone()[0]
            delay = max(0, next_request - now)
            # Do not hold an API worker in a long sleep during provider backoff.
            if delay > 5:
                raise SessionDataError(
                    "openf1_rate_limited",
                    "OpenF1 is cooling down. Retry after the indicated delay.",
                    503,
                    max(1, int(delay) + 1),
                )
            db.execute(
                "UPDATE budget SET next_request=? WHERE id=1",
                (max(now, next_request) + 2.1,),
            )
        if delay:
            time.sleep(delay)

    def _backoff(self, seconds):
        with self.connect() as db:
            db.execute(
                "UPDATE budget SET next_request=MAX(next_request, ?) WHERE id=1",
                (time.time() + seconds,),
            )

    def get(self, endpoint, params, ttl=7 * 86400):
        if endpoint not in ENDPOINTS:
            raise ValueError("Unsupported OpenF1 endpoint")
        key = hashlib.sha256(
            json.dumps([endpoint, params], sort_keys=True).encode()
        ).hexdigest()
        # Serialize cold identical queries; successful requests are reused on disk.
        with self.lock:
            with self.connect() as db:
                row = db.execute(
                    "SELECT expires, body FROM responses WHERE key=?", (key,)
                ).fetchone()
            if row and row[0] > time.time():
                try:
                    cached = json.loads(row[1])
                    if isinstance(cached, list) and all(
                        isinstance(r, dict) for r in cached
                    ):
                        return cached
                except ValueError:
                    pass
            self._reserve()
            try:
                response = requests.get(
                    f"{BASE_URL}/{endpoint}",
                    params=params,
                    timeout=(10, 30),
                    headers={
                        "Accept": "application/json",
                        "User-Agent": "F1EvidenceDashboard/1",
                    },
                )
            except requests.RequestException as exc:
                raise SessionDataError(
                    "openf1_network_error",
                    "OpenF1 could not be reached. Retry shortly.",
                    502,
                    20,
                ) from exc
            if response.status_code == 429:
                raw = response.headers.get("Retry-After", "60")
                try:
                    delay = int(raw)
                except ValueError:
                    try:
                        delay = int(
                            parsedate_to_datetime(raw).timestamp() - time.time()
                        )
                    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError):
                        delay = 60
                delay = min(max(delay, 1), 3600)
                self._backoff(delay)
                raise SessionDataError(
                    "openf1_rate_limited",
                    "OpenF1's free request limit was reached. Cached data remains available.",
                    503,
                    delay,
                )
            if response.status_code in (401, 403):
                raise SessionDataError(
                    "openf1_access_unavailable",
                    "OpenF1 denied this request. Only historical sessions outside the live window are supported without an account.",
                    503,
                    60,
                )
            if response.status_code == 404:
                raise SessionDataError(
                    "openf1_not_published",
                    f"OpenF1 has not published {endpoint} for this session.",
                    425,
                    60,
                )
            if response.status_code != 200:
                raise SessionDataError(
                    "openf1_upstream_error",
                    f"OpenF1 returned HTTP {response.status_code}. Retry shortly.",
                    502,
                    30,
                )
            try:
                body = response.json()
            except ValueError as exc:
                raise SessionDataError(
                    "openf1_invalid_response",
                    "OpenF1 returned an invalid JSON response.",
                    502,
                    30,
                ) from exc
            if not isinstance(body, list) or not all(isinstance(r, dict) for r in body):
                raise SessionDataError(
                    "openf1_invalid_response",
                    "OpenF1 returned an unexpected data format.",
                    502,
                    30,
                )
            # Empty data is not a permanent historical cache hit.
            with self.connect() as db:
                db.execute(
                    "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO responses VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                    (
                        key,
                        time.time() + (ttl if body else min(ttl, 60)),
                        json.dumps(body, allow_nan=False),
                    ),
                )
            return body


============================================================
FILE: ./backend/services/__init__.py
============================================================
"""Shared data and analysis services; CLI tools remain independently usable."""


============================================================
FILE: ./backend/services/openf1.py
============================================================
"""Free historical laps and lazy per-lap telemetry, in the dashboard's schema.

This implements only the FastF1-like operations used by this application. It
does not claim to recreate FastF1's accuracy flags or qualifying phase timing.
"""

from functools import lru_cache

import fastf1
from fastf1.core import Lap, Laps
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from .loading_errors import SessionDataError
from .openf1_client import OpenF1Client
from .openf1_quality import control_in_window, numeric, screen_laps, timestamp
from .results import load_results_session
from . import events

SESSION_NAMES = {
    "FP1": "Practice 1",
    "FP2": "Practice 2",
    "FP3": "Practice 3",
    "Q": "Qualifying",
    "R": "Race",
    "S": "Sprint",
    "SQ": "Sprint Qualifying",
    "SS": "Sprint Qualifying",
}
QUALITY_NOTE = (
    "OpenF1 screening differs from FastF1 IsAccurate: positive lap/sector times "
    "must agree within 0.02s; opening, pit, reported deleted and race-control "
    "hazard laps are excluded. Unknown coverage is excluded. Approximate lap "
    "boundaries, unreported incidents/deletions and traffic remain limitations. "
    "Replay selects the fastest remaining screened lap, which may differ from the official fastest lap."
)
TELEMETRY_NOTE = (
    "OpenF1 car positions are approximate (~3.7 Hz), not surveyed GPS. Position "
    "samples are matched within 0.5s of recorded car data. Distance is estimated "
    "by integrating speed, and lap start timestamps are approximate; replay and "
    "distance-aligned time deltas are illustrative. Brake is normalized to on/off. "
    "Missing DRS values (including 2026 aero data) are shown as gaps, not zero."
)


@lru_cache(maxsize=4)
def client(cache_dir):
    return OpenF1Client(cache_dir)


def resolve_session(api, year, template, kind):
    if year < 2023:
        raise SessionDataError(
            "openf1_year_unsupported",
            "OpenF1 historical timing starts in 2023. Earlier seasons require an existing FastF1 cache or optional archive access.",
            422,
        )
    name = SESSION_NAMES.get(kind)
    if name is None:
        raise SessionDataError(
            "openf1_session_unsupported", "Unsupported historical session type.", 422
        )
    start = timestamp(template.date)
    if pd.isna(start):
        raise SessionDataError(
            "session_date_unavailable",
            "No reliable session start date is available.",
            502,
        )
    if start > pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC"):
        raise SessionDataError(
            "session_not_started", "This session has not started yet.", 425, 60
        )
    query = {"year": year, "session_name": name}
    country = template.event.get("Country")
    if country and pd.notna(country):
        query["country_name"] = str(country)
    sessions = api.get("sessions", query, ttl=3600)
    candidates = [
        r
        for r in sessions
        if r.get("session_name") == name
        and abs(timestamp(r.get("date_start")) - start) < pd.Timedelta(hours=12)
    ]
    if len(candidates) != 1:
        raise SessionDataError(
            "openf1_session_unavailable",
            "OpenF1 has no unique matching session. It may not be published yet; no nearby race has been substituted.",
            425,
            60,
        )
    info = candidates[0]
    if info.get("is_cancelled"):
        raise SessionDataError("session_cancelled", "This session was cancelled.", 422)
    end = timestamp(info.get("date_end"))
    if pd.isna(end) or end + pd.Timedelta(minutes=30) >= pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC"):
        raise SessionDataError(
            "openf1_historical_pending",
            "Free OpenF1 data is available outside the live window. Retry at least 30 minutes after the session ends.",
            425,
            60,
        )
    return info


class HistoricalLap(Lap):
    def get_telemetry(self, **kwargs):
        return self.session.telemetry(self)


class HistoricalLaps(Laps):
    @property
    def _constructor_sliced_horizontal(self):
        return HistoricalLap

    def pick_accurate(self):
        # A compatibility selector, not an assertion of FastF1 IsAccurate.
        return self.loc[self["OpenF1Valid"]]

    def pick_track_status(self, status, how="equals"):
        if status == "1" and how == "equals":
            return self.loc[self["OpenF1Screened"]]
        return super().pick_track_status(status, how)

    def pick_fastest(self, only_by_time=False):
        sample = self.loc[self["OpenF1Eligible"] & self["LapTime"].notna()]
        return None if sample.empty else sample.loc[sample["LapTime"].idxmin()]


class HistoricalSession:
    data_provider = "openf1"
    data_source = "OpenF1 historical timing (public, 2023+)"

    def __init__(self, api, template, info):
        self.api, self.info = api, info
        self.event = template.event
        self.name = info["session_name"]
        # Existing legacy adapters compare naive UTC FastF1 session dates.
        self.date = timestamp(info["date_start"]).tz_localize(None)
        self.data_warnings = [QUALITY_NOTE]
        self.completed = False
        self.qualifying_classification = False
        self.classification_source = None

    @property
    def drivers(self):
        return self.results["DriverNumber"].astype(str).tolist()

    def get_driver(self, identifier):
        rows = self.results.loc[
            self.results["DriverNumber"].eq(str(identifier))
            | self.results["Abbreviation"].eq(str(identifier).upper())
        ]
        if rows.empty:
            raise ValueError("Driver not in this session")
        return rows.iloc[0]

    def telemetry(self, lap):
        start = timestamp(lap["LapStartDate"])
        duration = lap["LapTime"].total_seconds()
        # Never download an entire session's high-frequency data for a replay.
        params = {
            "session_key": self.info["session_key"],
            "driver_number": int(lap["DriverNumber"]),
            "date>": (start - pd.Timedelta(seconds=1)).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f"),
            "date<": (start + pd.Timedelta(seconds=duration + 1)).strftime(
                "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f"
            ),
        }
        car = self.api.get("car_data", params)
        pos = self.api.get("location", params)
        return merge_telemetry(car, pos, start, duration)


def merge_telemetry(car, positions, start, duration):
    def frame(rows, mapping):
        df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
        if df.empty or not {"date", *(key for key in mapping if key != "drs")}.issubset(
            df.columns
        ):
            raise SessionDataError(
                "openf1_telemetry_unavailable",
                "Recorded car/position channels are missing for this lap.",
                425,
                60,
            )
        if "drs" in mapping and "drs" not in df:
            df["drs"] = np.nan
        df = df.rename(columns={"date": "Date", **mapping})
        df["Date"] = pd.to_datetime(
            df["Date"], utc=True, errors="coerce", format="mixed"
        )
        for column in mapping.values():
            df[column] = pd.to_numeric(df[column], errors="coerce")
        df = (
            df.loc[:, ["Date", *mapping.values()]]
            .replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
            .dropna(subset=["Date", *(v for v in mapping.values() if v != "DRS")])
        )
        return df.sort_values("Date").drop_duplicates("Date")

    a = frame(
        car,
        {
            "speed": "Speed",
            "throttle": "Throttle",
            "brake": "Brake",
            "n_gear": "nGear",
            "drs": "DRS",
        },
    )
    b = frame(positions, {"x": "X", "y": "Y"})
    a = a.loc[a["Date"].between(start, start + pd.Timedelta(seconds=duration))]
    tel = pd.merge_asof(
        a, b, on="Date", direction="nearest", tolerance=pd.Timedelta(seconds=0.5)
    ).dropna(subset=["Date", "Speed", "Throttle", "Brake", "nGear", "X", "Y"])
    if len(tel) < max(20, duration * 2) or len(tel) < 0.95 * len(a):
        raise SessionDataError(
            "openf1_telemetry_incomplete",
            "This lap has insufficient overlapping recorded car and position samples.",
            425,
            60,
        )
    elapsed = (tel["Date"] - start).dt.total_seconds().to_numpy()
    if elapsed[0] > 1.5 or duration - elapsed[-1] > 1.5 or np.diff(elapsed).max() > 2:
        raise SessionDataError(
            "openf1_telemetry_incomplete",
            "This lap has a telemetry gap; missing movement has not been reconstructed.",
            425,
            60,
        )
    speed = tel["Speed"].to_numpy(dtype=float) / 3.6
    if (speed < 0).any() or not tel["Throttle"].between(0, 100).all():
        raise SessionDataError(
            "openf1_telemetry_invalid", "OpenF1 returned invalid telemetry values.", 502
        )
    tel = tel.copy()
    tel["Brake"] = tel["Brake"].gt(0).astype(int)
    tel["Time"] = pd.to_timedelta(elapsed, unit="s")
    tel["Distance"] = np.r_[
        0, np.cumsum((speed[:-1] + speed[1:]) * 0.5 * np.diff(elapsed))
    ]
    return tel.reset_index(drop=True)


def _optional(api, endpoint, params, warnings):
    try:
        return api.get(endpoint, params)
    except SessionDataError as exc:
        warnings.append(f"{endpoint} unavailable: {exc.detail}")
        return None


def _roster(drivers):
    frame = pd.DataFrame(drivers).rename(
        columns={
            "driver_number": "DriverNumber",
            "name_acronym": "Abbreviation",
            "team_name": "TeamName",
            "full_name": "FullName",
        }
    )
    required = {"DriverNumber", "Abbreviation", "TeamName"}
    if frame.empty or not required.issubset(frame.columns):
        raise SessionDataError(
            "openf1_roster_unavailable",
            "OpenF1's driver roster is not available.",
            425,
            60,
        )
    frame = frame.dropna(subset=list(required)).copy()
    frame["DriverNumber"] = frame["DriverNumber"].astype(int).astype(str)
    frame["Abbreviation"] = frame["Abbreviation"].astype(str).str.upper()
    frame["FullName"] = frame.get("FullName", frame["Abbreviation"])
    return frame.drop_duplicates("DriverNumber").set_index("DriverNumber", drop=False)


def _laps(rows, roster, stints, pits, session, controls):
    frame = pd.DataFrame(rows).rename(
        columns={
            "driver_number": "DriverNumber",
            "lap_number": "LapNumber",
            "date_start": "LapStartDate",
            "lap_duration": "LapTime",
            "duration_sector_1": "Sector1Time",
            "duration_sector_2": "Sector2Time",
            "duration_sector_3": "Sector3Time",
        }
    )
    required = {"DriverNumber", "LapNumber", "LapStartDate", "LapTime"}
    if frame.empty or not required.issubset(frame.columns):
        raise SessionDataError(
            "openf1_laps_unavailable",
            "OpenF1 has not published usable lap timing for this session.",
            425,
            60,
        )
    frame["DriverNumber"] = pd.to_numeric(frame["DriverNumber"], errors="coerce")
    frame["LapNumber"] = pd.to_numeric(frame["LapNumber"], errors="coerce")
    frame = frame.dropna(subset=["DriverNumber", "LapNumber"]).copy()
    frame["DriverNumber"] = frame["DriverNumber"].astype(int).astype(str)
    frame = frame.loc[frame["DriverNumber"].isin(roster.index)].copy()
    # Duplicate or fractional lap identities cannot safely be paired.
    frame = frame.loc[frame["LapNumber"].gt(0) & frame["LapNumber"].mod(1).eq(0)]
    frame = frame.drop_duplicates(["DriverNumber", "LapNumber"], keep=False)
    frame["LapStartDate"] = pd.to_datetime(
        frame["LapStartDate"], utc=True, errors="coerce", format="mixed"
    )
    start, end = timestamp(session.info["date_start"]), timestamp(
        session.info["date_end"]
    )
    frame = frame.loc[
        frame["LapStartDate"].between(
            start - pd.Timedelta(minutes=30), end + pd.Timedelta(hours=6)
        )
    ].copy()
    for column in ("LapTime", "Sector1Time", "Sector2Time", "Sector3Time"):
        values = pd.to_numeric(
            frame.get(column, pd.Series(np.nan, index=frame.index)), errors="coerce"
        )
        frame[column] = pd.to_timedelta(values.where(np.isfinite(values)), unit="s")
    frame["Driver"] = frame["DriverNumber"].map(roster["Abbreviation"])
    frame["Team"] = frame["DriverNumber"].map(roster["TeamName"])
    frame["Compound"], frame["TyreLife"], frame["Stint"] = None, np.nan, np.nan
    ambiguous_stint = pd.Series(False, index=frame.index)
    # Missing accuracy/track flags remain unknown, never fabricated FastF1 flags.
    frame["IsAccurate"] = pd.Series(pd.NA, index=frame.index, dtype="boolean")
    frame["TrackStatus"] = "UNKNOWN"
    for column in ("PitInTime", "PitOutTime"):
        frame[column] = pd.Series(pd.NaT, index=frame.index, dtype="timedelta64[ns]")
    frame["OpenF1PitOutKnown"] = frame.get(
        "is_pit_out_lap", pd.Series(None, index=frame.index)
    ).map(lambda v: isinstance(v, (bool, np.bool_)))
    out = frame.get("is_pit_out_lap", pd.Series(None, index=frame.index)).eq(True)
    frame.loc[out, "PitOutTime"] = pd.Timedelta(0)
    for p in pits or []:
        driver, lap = p.get("driver_number"), numeric(p.get("lap_number"))
        if driver is not None and np.isfinite(lap):
            # Exclude both sides of the stop even when only pit lap is supplied.
            selected = frame["DriverNumber"].eq(str(driver))
            frame.loc[selected & frame["LapNumber"].eq(lap), "PitInTime"] = (
                pd.Timedelta(0)
            )
            frame.loc[selected & frame["LapNumber"].eq(lap + 1), "PitOutTime"] = (
                pd.Timedelta(0)
            )
    for s in stints or []:
        driver = s.get("driver_number")
        lo, hi = numeric(s.get("lap_start")), numeric(s.get("lap_end"))
        if driver is None or not np.isfinite(lo) or not np.isfinite(hi):
            continue
        selected = frame["DriverNumber"].eq(str(driver)) & frame["LapNumber"].between(
            lo, hi
        )
        # Overlapping upstream stint ranges are ambiguous; exclude from matching.
        overlap = selected & frame["Stint"].notna()
        ambiguous_stint |= overlap
        frame.loc[selected, "Compound"] = s.get("compound")
        frame.loc[selected, "Stint"] = numeric(s.get("stint_number"))
        frame.loc[selected, "TyreLife"] = (
            numeric(s.get("tyre_age_at_start"))
            + frame.loc[selected, "LapNumber"]
            - lo
            + 1
        )
    frame.loc[ambiguous_stint, "Compound"] = None
    frame.loc[ambiguous_stint, ["Stint", "TyreLife"]] = np.nan
    frame.loc[
        ~frame["Compound"].isin(["SOFT", "MEDIUM", "HARD", "INTERMEDIATE", "WET"]),
        "Compound",
    ] = None
    frame.loc[frame["TyreLife"].lt(1) | frame["TyreLife"].mod(1).ne(0), "TyreLife"] = (
        np.nan
    )
    frame["Time"] = (frame["LapStartDate"] - start) + frame["LapTime"]
    frame["LapStartTime"] = frame["LapStartDate"] - start
    frame = screen_laps(frame, controls, end, pit_available=pits is not None)
    return HistoricalLaps(frame.reset_index(drop=True), session=session)


def load_session(year, event, kind, cache_dir, template=None):
    template = template or events.get_session(year, event, kind)
    api = client(str(cache_dir))
    info = resolve_session(api, year, template, kind)
    session = HistoricalSession(api, template, info)
    params = {"session_key": info["session_key"]}
    session.results = _roster(api.get("drivers", params))
    rows = api.get("laps", params)
    if not rows:
        raise SessionDataError(
            "openf1_laps_unavailable",
            "OpenF1 lap timing is not published yet.",
            425,
            60,
        )
    warnings = session.data_warnings
    stints = _optional(api, "stints", params, warnings)
    pits = _optional(api, "pit", params, warnings)
    controls = _optional(api, "race_control", params, warnings)
    controls = control_in_window(
        controls or [], timestamp(info["date_start"]), timestamp(info["date_end"])
    )
    session.race_control_messages = pd.DataFrame(controls)
    finishes = [
        timestamp(r["date"])
        for r in controls
        if r.get("category") == "SessionStatus"
        and r.get("message") in ("SESSION FINISHED", "SESSION ENDED")
        and timestamp(r["date"]) >= timestamp(info["date_start"])
    ]
    if finishes and max(finishes) + pd.Timedelta(minutes=30) >= pd.Timestamp.now(
        tz="UTC"
    ):
        raise SessionDataError(
            "openf1_historical_pending",
            "The delayed session ended recently; retry 30 minutes after its actual finish.",
            425,
            60,
        )
    session.completed = any(
        r.get("category") == "SessionStatus"
        and r.get("message") in ("SESSION FINISHED", "SESSION ENDED")
        and timestamp(r["date"]) >= timestamp(info["date_start"])
        for r in controls
    )
    for column in ("Q1", "Q2", "Q3"):
        session.results[column] = pd.Series(
            pd.NaT, index=session.results.index, dtype="timedelta64[ns]"
        )
    session.results["Position"] = np.nan
    public = None
    if kind in ("Q", "R", "S"):
        try:
            public = load_results_session(year, event, kind)
        except SessionDataError as exc:
            warnings.append(
                f"Published Jolpica classification unavailable: {exc.detail}"
            )
    if public is not None:
        for column in public.results.columns:
            if column not in ("DriverNumber", "Abbreviation", "TeamName", "FullName"):
                session.results[column] = session.results["DriverNumber"].map(
                    public.results.set_index("DriverNumber")[column]
                )
        session.completed = True
        session.qualifying_classification = kind == "Q"
        session.classification_source = public.data_source
    else:
        classification = _optional(api, "session_result", params, warnings)
        if classification:
            positions = {
                str(r.get("driver_number")): numeric(r.get("position"))
                for r in classification
            }
            session.results["Position"] = session.results["DriverNumber"].map(positions)
            session.completed = True
            session.classification_source = "OpenF1 published session result"
    session.results = session.results.sort_values("Position", na_position="last")
    session.laps = _laps(rows, session.results, stints, pits, session, controls)
    if session.laps.empty or not session.laps["LapTime"].notna().any():
        raise SessionDataError(
            "openf1_laps_unavailable",
            "No finite recorded laps are available from OpenF1.",
            425,
            60,
        )
    if not stints:
        warnings.append(
            "Tyre stints unavailable: compound/age comparisons are omitted."
        )
    if not controls:
        warnings.append(
            "Race-control coverage unavailable: screened pace samples are omitted."
        )
    warnings.append(
        "SC/VSC laps remain excluded until an explicit track-wide GREEN message; uncertain restarts can reduce samples."
    )
    warnings.append(
        f"OpenF1 screening retained {int(session.laps['OpenF1Eligible'].sum())} of {len(session.laps)} recorded laps."
    )
    weather = _optional(api, "weather", params, warnings)
    session.weather_data = pd.DataFrame(weather or []).rename(
        columns={
            "air_temperature": "AirTemp",
            "track_temperature": "TrackTemp",
            "humidity": "Humidity",
            "wind_speed": "WindSpeed",
            "rainfall": "Rainfall",
        }
    )
    if "date" in session.weather_data:
        dates = pd.to_datetime(
            session.weather_data["date"], utc=True, errors="coerce", format="mixed"
        )
        observed_end = max([timestamp(info["date_end"]), *finishes])
        session.weather_data = session.weather_data.loc[
            dates.between(timestamp(info["date_start"]), observed_end)
        ].copy()
    else:
        session.weather_data = session.weather_data.iloc[:0].copy()
    for column in ("AirTemp", "TrackTemp", "Humidity", "WindSpeed", "Rainfall"):
        if column not in session.weather_data:
            session.weather_data[column] = np.nan
        session.weather_data[column] = pd.to_numeric(
            session.weather_data[column], errors="coerce"
        )
    return session


============================================================
FILE: ./backend/services/jobs.py
============================================================
"""Bounded in-process read jobs for season analyses; predictions stay persistent."""

import threading
import time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from uuid import uuid4

POOL = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1, thread_name_prefix="f1-analysis")
LOCK = threading.Lock()
JOBS = {}


def start(key, work):
    with LOCK:
        now = time.time()
        for job in JOBS.values():
            if job["key"] == key and (
                job["status"] == "running"
                or (job["status"] == "complete" and now - job["updated"] < 3600)
            ):
                return public(job)
        expired = [
            k
            for k, v in JOBS.items()
            if v["status"] != "running" and now - v["updated"] > 3600
        ]
        for item in expired:
            del JOBS[item]
        if len(JOBS) >= 10:
            complete = [
                (j["updated"], k) for k, j in JOBS.items() if j["status"] != "running"
            ]
            if not complete:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Analysis queue is full; retry when another season finishes"
                )
            del JOBS[min(complete)[1]]
        job = {
            "id": str(uuid4()),
            "key": key,
            "status": "running",
            "completed": 0,
            "total": 0,
            "data": None,
            "error": None,
            "updated": now,
        }
        JOBS[job["id"]] = job

    def progress(done, total):
        with LOCK:
            job.update(completed=done, total=total, updated=time.time())

    def run():
        try:
            result = work(progress)
            with LOCK:
                job.update(data=result, status="complete", updated=time.time())
        except Exception as exc:
            from fastapi import HTTPException

            message = (
                str(exc.detail)
                if isinstance(exc, HTTPException)
                else "Season data could not be loaded. Retry later."
            )
            with LOCK:
                job.update(
                    error=message,
                    status="failed",
                    updated=time.time(),
                )

    POOL.submit(run)
    return public(job)


def public(job):
    return {k: v for k, v in job.items() if k not in ("key", "updated")}


def get(job_id):
    with LOCK:
        return public(JOBS[job_id]) if job_id in JOBS else None


============================================================
FILE: ./backend/services/empirical_model.py
============================================================
"""Versioned winner model: numeric evidence only, chronological validation.

Artifacts are JSON coefficients (never untrusted pickle). No driver or team IDs
are training features. Training is an explicit offline command, not a public API.
"""

import hashlib
import json
import os
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler

from .data import (
    DATA_DIR,
    clean_laps,
    finished,
    iso,
    load_session,
    number,
    seconds,
    utc_now,
)

MODEL_DIR = DATA_DIR / "models"
FEATURES = [
    "qualifyingGapPercent",
    "qualifyingRankFraction",
    "practiceRelativePacePercent",
    "practiceMissing",
]


def features(year, event, session_type="Q"):
    session = load_session(year, event, session_type, results_only=session_type == "Q")
    cutoff = pd.Timestamp(session.date)
    if cutoff.tzinfo is None:
        cutoff = cutoff.tz_localize("UTC")
    if cutoff >= pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC") or not finished(session):
        raise ValueError("Select a completed session with published timing")
    rows = session.results.copy()
    rows = rows.loc[rows["Abbreviation"].notna() & rows["Abbreviation"].ne("")]
    rows = rows.sort_values("Position", na_position="last")
    if rows.empty:
        raise ValueError("No driver results")
    field = len(rows)
    # All Q comparisons use Q1; no Q1/Q3 mix caused by track evolution.
    times = {}
    if session_type == "Q":
        for _, row in rows.iterrows():
            times[str(row["Abbreviation"])] = seconds(row.get("Q1"))
    else:
        clean = clean_laps(session.laps)
        for driver, df in clean.groupby("Driver"):
            times[str(driver)] = number(df["seconds"].min())
    valid = [v for v in times.values() if v is not None and v > 0]
    if not valid:
        raise ValueError("No valid timed laps for prediction features")
    best = min(valid)
    practice, practice_kind = None, None
    # Never load a later practice session into an earlier-session prediction.
    for kind in ("FP3", "FP2", "FP1"):
        try:
            candidate = session.event.get_session(kind)
            start = pd.Timestamp(candidate.date)
            if start.tzinfo is None:
                start = start.tz_localize("UTC")
            if start <= cutoff:
                candidate = load_session(year, event, kind)
                if finished(candidate):
                    practice, practice_kind = candidate, kind
                    break
        except Exception:
            continue
    relative = {}
    if practice is not None:
        clean = clean_laps(practice.laps)
        # Long-run samples; compare relative to compound's field median.
        if not clean.empty:
            clean = clean.groupby(["Driver", "Stint"], group_keys=False).filter(
                lambda group: len(group) >= 3
            )
            if not clean.empty:
                benchmark = clean.groupby("Compound")["seconds"].transform("median")
                clean["relative"] = 100 * (clean["seconds"] / benchmark - 1)
                relative = clean.groupby("Driver")["relative"].median().to_dict()
    entries = []
    for i, (_, row) in enumerate(rows.iterrows()):
        driver = str(row["Abbreviation"])
        lap = times.get(driver)
        entries.append(
            {
                "driver": driver,
                "team": str(row["TeamName"]),
                "values": [
                    (
                        100 * (lap / best - 1)
                        if lap is not None
                        else max(5.0, 100 * (max(valid) / best - 1))
                    ),
                    i / max(field - 1, 1),
                    float(relative.get(driver, 0)),
                    0.0 if driver in relative else 1.0,
                ],
            }
        )
    return (
        session,
        entries,
        {
            "cutoff": cutoff.isoformat(),
            "practiceSession": practice_kind,
            "practiceSource": getattr(practice, "data_source", None),
            "dataWarnings": (
                list(getattr(practice, "data_warnings", []))
                if practice is not None
                else [
                    "No pre-cutoff raw practice data is available. Practice features use the model's explicit missing-data indicator."
                ]
            ),
            "qualifyingSource": getattr(session, "data_source", "FastF1"),
            "featureNames": FEATURES,
            "qualifyingBasis": "Q1" if session_type == "Q" else "practice best lap",
        },
    )


def infer(artifact, entries):
    if artifact["featureNames"] != FEATURES:
        raise ValueError("Model feature schema mismatch; retrain this artifact")
    x = np.array([row["values"] for row in entries], dtype=float)
    score = ((x - np.array(artifact["mean"])) / np.array(artifact["scale"])) @ np.array(
        artifact["coef"]
    ) + artifact["intercept"]
    # Logistic one-vs-rest scores normalized over this event's actual field.
    logits = 1 / (1 + np.exp(-np.clip(score, -40, 40)))
    return logits / logits.sum()


def metrics(events, predictions):
    if not events:
        raise ValueError("No complete events to evaluate")
    brier, loss, hits = [], [], []
    for event, probs in zip(events, predictions):
        y = np.array(
            [1.0 if e["driver"] == event["winner"] else 0.0 for e in event["entries"]]
        )
        brier.append(float(np.sum((probs - y) ** 2)))
        winner_index = int(np.argmax(y))
        loss.append(float(-np.log(max(float(probs[winner_index]), 1e-15))))
        hits.append(int(np.argmax(probs)) == winner_index)
    return {
        "events": len(events),
        "brier": number(np.mean(brier)),
        "logLoss": number(np.mean(loss)),
        "top1Accuracy": number(np.mean(hits)),
    }


def fit(events, c):
    x = np.array([entry["values"] for event in events for entry in event["entries"]])
    y = np.array(
        [
            int(entry["driver"] == event["winner"])
            for event in events
            for entry in event["entries"]
        ]
    )
    scaler = StandardScaler().fit(x)
    model = LogisticRegression(C=c, max_iter=2000, random_state=17).fit(
        scaler.transform(x), y
    )
    return {
        "featureNames": FEATURES,
        "mean": scaler.mean_.tolist(),
        "scale": scaler.scale_.tolist(),
        "coef": model.coef_[0].tolist(),
        "intercept": float(model.intercept_[0]),
        "regularizationC": c,
    }


def artifacts():
    MODEL_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    result = []
    for path in sorted(MODEL_DIR.glob("v13-*.json")):
        try:
            artifact = json.loads(path.read_text())
            if artifact["id"] == path.stem:
                result.append(artifact)
        except (ValueError, KeyError):
            continue
    return sorted(result, key=lambda r: (r["trainedThrough"], r["createdAt"]))


def train(year_from, year_to, through_round=None, min_events=15):
    import fastf1

    events, skipped = [], []
    for year in range(year_from, year_to + 1):
        from .events import get_schedule

        schedule = get_schedule(year, include_testing=False)
        for _, event in schedule.iterrows():
            rd = int(event["RoundNumber"])
            if year == year_to and through_round is not None and rd > through_round:
                continue
            if (
                pd.Timestamp(event["EventDate"]).date()
                >= datetime.now(timezone.utc).date()
            ):
                continue
            try:
                qualifying, entries, meta = features(year, rd, "Q")
                race = load_session(year, rd, "R", results_only=True)
                if not finished(race):
                    raise ValueError("Race not finished")
                results = race.results.sort_values("Position")
                winner = str(results.iloc[0]["Abbreviation"])
                if winner not in {e["driver"] for e in entries}:
                    raise ValueError("Winner missing from pre-race field")
                # Require label availability after the race, not just its start.
                # A published classification is enough for labels; don't require
                # protected raw laps. Use a conservative next-day availability
                # boundary when exact result publication time is unobserved.
                race_start = pd.Timestamp(race.date)
                if race_start.tzinfo is None:
                    race_start = race_start.tz_localize("UTC")
                label_cutoff = race_start + pd.Timedelta(days=1)
                if label_cutoff > pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC"):
                    raise ValueError("Result publication buffer has not elapsed")
                events.append(
                    {
                        "key": f"{year}-{rd}",
                        "year": year,
                        "round": rd,
                        "winner": winner,
                        "entries": entries,
                        "cutoff": meta["cutoff"],
                        "labelCutoff": label_cutoff.isoformat(),
                        "qualifyingSource": meta.get("qualifyingSource"),
                        "practiceSource": meta.get("practiceSource"),
                    }
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                skipped.append({"event": f"{year}-{rd}", "reason": str(exc)})
    events.sort(key=lambda e: e["cutoff"])
    if len(events) < min_events:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Need {min_events} complete races; loaded {len(events)}. Skipped: {skipped}"
        )
    first, second = int(len(events) * 0.6), int(len(events) * 0.8)
    train_events, validation, test = (
        events[:first],
        events[first:second],
        events[second:],
    )
    candidates = []
    for c in (0.1, 1.0, 10.0):
        artifact = fit(train_events, c)
        score = metrics(validation, [infer(artifact, e["entries"]) for e in validation])
        candidates.append((score["logLoss"], c))
    best_c = min(candidates)[1]
    heldout_model = fit(train_events + validation, best_c)
    heldout = metrics(test, [infer(heldout_model, e["entries"]) for e in test])
    uniform = metrics(
        test, [np.ones(len(e["entries"])) / len(e["entries"]) for e in test]
    )
    # Fixed comparator: 50% to first qualifying position, balance to other drivers.
    grid = metrics(
        test,
        [
            np.array([0.5] + [0.5 / (len(e["entries"]) - 1)] * (len(e["entries"]) - 1))
            for e in test
        ],
    )
    prior = artifacts()
    prior_score = None
    if prior and prior[-1]["trainedThrough"] < test[0]["cutoff"]:
        prior_score = metrics(test, [infer(prior[-1], e["entries"]) for e in test])
    artifact = fit(events, best_c)
    manifest = {
        "events": [e["key"] for e in events],
        "cutoff": events[-1]["labelCutoff"],
        "features": FEATURES,
        "c": best_c,
        "algorithmVersion": 2,
        "timingPolicy": "OpenF1 screened historical laps or FastF1 validated cache/archive",
        "datasetHash": hashlib.sha256(
            json.dumps(events, sort_keys=True).encode()
        ).hexdigest(),
    }
    digest = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(manifest, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()[
        :12
    ]
    artifact.update(
        id=f"v13-{digest}",
        name="Empirical winner model",
        version=13,
        createdAt=utc_now(),
        trainedThrough=max(e["labelCutoff"] for e in events),
        trainingEvents=len(events),
        trainingEventKeys=manifest["events"],
        heldoutEventKeys=[e["key"] for e in test],
        metrics=heldout,
        baselines={"uniform": uniform, "qualifyingLeader50Percent": grid},
        previousComparable=prior_score,
        improvesGridLogLoss=heldout["logLoss"] < grid["logLoss"],
        improvements=[
            "Learned numeric coefficients; no driver names or team tiers",
            "Whole-race chronological 60/20/20 split; regularization chosen only on validation",
            "Untouched test scores reported before final refit on all included races",
        ],
        limitations=[
            "Small observational sample; improvement is not guaranteed",
            "Practice fuel, traffic and setup are unobserved",
            "Test scores describe the held-out fit, not the final refit",
        ],
        skipped=skipped,
    )
    MODEL_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    path = MODEL_DIR / f'{artifact["id"]}.json'
    # One immutable iteration per training manifest; reruns reuse its existing audit record.
    if path.exists():
        return json.loads(path.read_text())
    temp = MODEL_DIR / f".{artifact['id']}-{uuid.uuid4().hex}.tmp"
    temp.write_text(json.dumps(artifact, indent=2, allow_nan=False))
    try:
        # Link creation is atomic and cannot replace an existing iteration.
        os.link(temp, path)
    except FileExistsError:
        return json.loads(path.read_text())
    finally:
        temp.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    return artifact

FILE: ./backend/services/openf1_quality.py
============================================================
"""Conservative OpenF1 screening, deliberately distinct from FastF1 accuracy.

Race-control screening is not a complete track-status or deleted-lap feed.
Unknown status, missing sectors, and missing pit coverage fail closed.
"""

import re

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd


def timestamp(value):
    return pd.to_datetime(value, utc=True, errors="coerce")


def numeric(value):
    try:
        value = float(value)
        return value if np.isfinite(value) else np.nan
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return np.nan


def control_in_window(rows, start, end):
    # Some upstream responses include messages from another day/session.
    return sorted(
        [
            r
            for r in rows
            if start - pd.Timedelta(hours=2)
            <= timestamp(r.get("date"))
            <= end + pd.Timedelta(hours=6)
        ],
        key=lambda r: timestamp(r["date"]),
    )


def screened_intervals(messages, end):
    """Intervals observed running without an outstanding hazard message.

    Sector CLEAR cannot clear a safety car or red flag. SC IN THIS LAP / LIGHTS
    OFF is not a green restart. Some usable laps will intentionally be excluded.
    """
    known, stopped, safety, sectors, track_flag = False, False, False, set(), False
    intervals, since = [], None
    for r in messages:
        date = timestamp(r.get("date"))
        message = str(r.get("message") or "").upper()
        flag = str(r.get("flag") or "").upper()
        category = str(r.get("category") or "")
        scope = str(r.get("scope") or "").upper()
        if since is not None:
            intervals.append((since, date))
            since = None
        if category == "SessionStatus":
            if message == "SESSION STARTED":
                known, stopped = True, False
            elif message in (
                "SESSION ABORTED",
                "SESSION SUSPENDED",
                "SESSION FINISHED",
                "SESSION ENDED",
            ):
                stopped = True
        if category == "SafetyCar" and "DEPLOYED" in message:
            safety = True
        if flag == "RED":
            stopped = True
        elif flag in ("YELLOW", "DOUBLE YELLOW"):
            if scope == "SECTOR" and r.get("sector") is not None:
                sectors.add(str(r["sector"]))
            else:
                track_flag = True
        elif flag in ("GREEN", "CLEAR"):
            if scope == "SECTOR" and r.get("sector") is not None:
                sectors.discard(str(r["sector"]))
            elif scope == "TRACK":
                track_flag = False
                if flag == "GREEN":
                    known, stopped, safety = True, False, False
                else:
                    sectors.clear()
        if known and not (stopped or safety or sectors or track_flag):
            since = date
    if since is not None:
        intervals.append((since, end + pd.Timedelta(hours=6)))
    # Merge adjacent good intervals; informational messages shouldn't split laps.
    merged = []
    for a, b in intervals:
        if merged and merged[-1][1] == a:
            merged[-1] = (merged[-1][0], b)
        else:
            merged.append((a, b))
    return merged


def deleted_laps(messages, laps):
    deleted, unknown_drivers = set(), set()
    for row in messages:
        message = str(row.get("message") or "").upper()
        if "DELETED" not in message:
            continue
        car = re.search(r"\bCAR\s+(\d+)\b", message)
        driver = str(car.group(1)) if car else row.get("driver_number")
        if driver is None:
            # A deletion which cannot be assigned invalidates the sample,
            # rather than silently accepting potentially deleted laps.
            unknown_drivers.update(laps["DriverNumber"].unique())
            continue
        driver = str(driver)
        lap = re.search(r"\bLAP\s+(\d+)\b", message)
        if lap:
            # The structured lap_number is often the leader's lap at message
            # publication, not the affected driver's deleted lap.
            deleted.add((driver, int(lap.group(1))))
            continue
        clock = re.search(r"\bTIME\s+(\d+):(\d{2}\.\d+)\b", message)
        if clock:
            seconds = int(clock.group(1)) * 60 + float(clock.group(2))
            match = laps.loc[
                laps["DriverNumber"].eq(driver)
                & (laps["LapTime"].dt.total_seconds() - seconds).abs().lt(0.001)
            ]
            if len(match) == 1:
                deleted.add((driver, int(match.iloc[0]["LapNumber"])))
                continue
        unknown_drivers.add(driver)
    return deleted, unknown_drivers


def screen_laps(laps, messages, end, pit_available):
    intervals = screened_intervals(messages, end)
    deleted, unknown_drivers = deleted_laps(messages, laps)
    valid, green = [], []
    for row in laps.itertuples():
        start, finish = row.LapStartDate, row.LapStartDate + row.LapTime
        # Pad approximate lap boundaries to exclude nearby hazard transitions.
        screened = any(
            a <= start - pd.Timedelta(seconds=1)
            and finish + pd.Timedelta(seconds=1) <= b
            for a, b in intervals
        )
        green.append(screened)
        sectors = [row.Sector1Time, row.Sector2Time, row.Sector3Time]
        consistent = all(pd.notna(s) and s > pd.Timedelta(0) for s in sectors)
        consistent = (
            consistent
            and pd.notna(row.LapTime)
            and abs((sum(sectors, pd.Timedelta(0)) - row.LapTime).total_seconds())
            <= 0.02
        )
        valid.append(
            bool(
                pit_available
                and consistent
                and pd.notna(start)
                and row.LapNumber > 1
                and row.OpenF1PitOutKnown
                and pd.isna(row.PitInTime)
                and pd.isna(row.PitOutTime)
                and row.DriverNumber not in unknown_drivers
                and (row.DriverNumber, int(row.LapNumber)) not in deleted
            )
        )
    laps["OpenF1Valid"] = valid
    laps["OpenF1Screened"] = green
    laps["OpenF1Eligible"] = laps["OpenF1Valid"] & laps["OpenF1Screened"]
    return laps

"""Create an auditable model iteration: python -m backend.train_predictor --help."""

import argparse
import json
from .services.empirical_model import train

if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--from-year", type=int, default=2023)
    parser.add_argument("--through-year", type=int, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--through-round", type=int)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.from_year > args.through_year:
        parser.error("from-year must not exceed through-year")
    result = train(args.from_year, args.through_year, args.through_round)
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                key: result[key]
                for key in (
                    "id",
                    "trainingEvents",
                    "trainedThrough",
                    "metrics",
                    "baselines",
                    "improvesGridLogLoss",
                    "skipped",
                )
            },
            indent=2,
        )
    )

"""Cited engineering context, separated from measured on-track outcomes."""

from fastapi import APIRouter

router = APIRouter()

TOPICS = {
    "2025": [
        {
            "id": "brakes",
            "title": "McLaren MCL39 · Brake cooling and tyre temperature",
            "documented": "F1 technical reporting links McLaren's rear brake-duct approach with tyre temperature control, while noting that its internal configuration was not widely known.",
            "measure": "Compare matched-compound race pace and adjusted lap-time trends, with track temperature shown for context.",
            "cannotInfer": "Timing does not measure brake-disc or tyre carcass temperature, airflow rate, or how many seconds this design caused.",
            "sourceLabel": "Formula 1 technical analysis · 20 May 2025",
            "sourceUrl": "https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article/tech-weekly-mclarens-ingenious-design-is-the-latest-step-in-a-spectacular.1zl8gcMuEAgJ1r2zefjrVb",
        },
    ],
    "2023": [
        {
            "id": "floor",
            "title": "Red Bull RB19 · Floor and aerodynamic platform",
            "documented": "F1's launch analysis identifies revised floor edges following the 2023 floor-height regulation changes. Exact underfloor performance cannot be read from public lap times.",
            "measure": "Inspect speed traces, throttle application and corner-speed differences at the same event; compare normalized qualifying advantage across rounds.",
            "cannotInfer": "No public downforce map, ride-height trace or floor pressure data is available here. A speed advantage alone does not isolate the floor.",
            "sourceLabel": "Formula 1 technical analysis · 28 February 2023",
            "sourceUrl": "https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article/tech-tuesday-dissecting-the-design-secrets-of-the-rb19-and-why-red-bulls.359CpeSh3M73afoQ5hEVfr",

    ],

        {
            "id": "suspension",
            "title": "Mercedes W11 · Suspension and aero packaging",
            "documented": "Mercedes' launch description explains that the rear suspension layout created further aerodynamic development opportunities.",
            "measure": "Compare corner entry, minimum speed, exit acceleration and qualifying advantage at the same circuit.",
            "cannotInfer": "Public telemetry does not isolate suspension geometry, damper settings or its individual lap-time contribution.",

def topics():

    }

import fastf1
from fastapi import APIRouter
from ..services.f1_access import MIN_VERSION, auth_status
from ..services.data import timing_provider

router = APIRouter()


@router.get("/status")
def data_status():
    return {
        "fastf1Version": fastf1.__version__,
        "minimumVersion": MIN_VERSION,
        "authentication": auth_status(),
        "timingProvider": timing_provider(),
        "historicalAccess": {
            "requiresAccount": False,
            "firstOpenF1Season": 2023,
            "liveWindowMinutesAfterSession": 30,
        },
        "sources": {
            "classifications": "Jolpica-F1 (public)",
            "timing": "Existing FastF1 cache, then free OpenF1 historical timing (2023+); authenticated archive is optional",
        },
    }


============================================================
FILE: ./backend/routers/weekend.py
============================================================
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from ..services.weekend import weekend

router = APIRouter()


@router.get("/{year}/{round_number}")
def get_weekend(year: int, round_number: int):
    try:
        return weekend(year, round_number)
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(404, str(exc)) from exc
    except Exception as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            502,
            "Weekend timing data could not be analysed. Retry when the data is published.",
        ) from exc

"""
Best Car Analysis API routes.
Provides car dominance metrics, performance comparisons, and season progression.
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import fastf1
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from typing import Optional, List
from pathlib import Path

router = APIRouter()

from ..services.data import load_session, CACHE_DIR

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# ERA configurations – mirrors formula1-best-car/data_loader.py
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
ERAS = {
    "2020": {
        "name": "2020 – Mercedes W11 Era",
        "year": 2020,
        "primaryCar": {
            "team": "Mercedes",
            "car": "W11",
            "drivers": ["HAM", "BOT"],
            "color": "#00D2BE",
        },
        "competitors": [
            {
                "team": "Red Bull",
                "car": "RB16",
                "drivers": ["VER", "ALB"],
                "color": "#0600EF",
            },
            {
                "team": "Racing Point",
                "car": "RP20",
                "drivers": ["PER", "STR"],
                "color": "#F596C8",
            },
        ],
    },
    "2023": {
        "name": "2023 – Red Bull RB19 Era",
        "year": 2023,
        "primaryCar": {
            "team": "Red Bull Racing",
            "car": "RB19",
            "drivers": ["VER", "PER"],
            "color": "#3671C6",
        },
        "competitors": [
            {
                "team": "Mercedes",
                "car": "W14",

def _load_session(year, race, session_type):
    return load_session(year, race, session_type, results_only=session_type == "Q")

@router.get("/eras")

@router.get("/era/{era_key}")
