    """Return full config for an era."""
    if era_key not in ERAS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Era '{era_key}' not found")
    return ERAS[era_key]


@router.get("/gap-to-leader/{year}/{race}")
def gap_to_leader(year: int, race: str):
    """Qualifying gap-to-leader for a specific race."""
    try:
        session = _load_session(year, race, "Q")
        # Q1 is a common timing segment; do not compare a Q1 lap to a Q3 lap.
        fastest_laps = []
        for _, row in session.results.iterrows():
            if pd.notna(row.get("Q1")):
                fastest_laps.append(
                    {
                        "driver": str(row["Abbreviation"]),
                        "team": str(row["TeamName"]),
                        "lapTime": pd.Timedelta(row["Q1"]).total_seconds(),
                    }
                )

        if not fastest_laps:
            return {"gaps": []}

        fastest_laps.sort(key=lambda x: x["lapTime"])
        leader = fastest_laps[0]["lapTime"]
        for item in fastest_laps:
            item["gap"] = round(item["lapTime"] - leader, 3)
            item["gapPercent"] = round((item["gap"] / leader) * 100, 3) if leader else 0

        return {"race": race, "year": year, "gaps": fastest_laps}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


def _season_progression(era_key: str, progress=None):
    """
    Track qualifying gap-to-leader across the season for the primary team.
    Returns list of {round, race, gap} entries.
    """
    if era_key not in ERAS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Era not found")

    era = ERAS[era_key]
    year = era["year"]
    drivers = era["primaryCar"]["drivers"]

    try:
        from ..services.events import get_schedule

        schedule = get_schedule(year)
        races = schedule[schedule["EventFormat"] != "testing"]["EventName"].tolist()
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

    progression, unavailable = [], []
    for i, race_name in enumerate(races):
        if progress:
            progress(i, len(races))
        try:
            session = _load_session(year, race_name, "Q")
            rows = session.results.dropna(subset=["Q1"])
            primary = rows[rows["TeamName"].eq(era["primaryCar"]["team"])]
            rivals = rows[~rows["TeamName"].eq(era["primaryCar"]["team"])]
            if primary.empty or rivals.empty:
                unavailable.append(race_name)
                continue
            p = primary["Q1"].min().total_seconds()
            r = rivals["Q1"].min().total_seconds()
            progression.append(
                {
                    "round": int(session.event["RoundNumber"]),
                    "race": race_name,
                    "gap": round(p - r, 4),
                    "gapPercent": round(100 * (p / r - 1), 4),
                }
            )
        except HTTPException as exc:
            if exc.status_code >= 500:
                raise
            unavailable.append(race_name)
        except Exception:
            unavailable.append(race_name)
    if progress:
        progress(len(races), len(races))
    return {
        "eraKey": era_key,
        "team": era["primaryCar"]["team"],
        "progression": progression,
        "unavailable": unavailable,
        "method": "Best primary driver minus fastest other-team Q1 time; negative is an advantage. Q1 controls segment, not track conditions. Percent gaps improve scale comparability, not causal attribution.",
    }


@router.get("/race-pace/{year}/{race}")
def race_pace(year: int, race: str):
    """Return average race pace per driver for a given race."""
    try:
        session = _load_session(year, race, "R")
        pace_data = []

        for drv in session.drivers:
            from ..services.data import clean_laps

            dl = clean_laps(session.laps.pick_drivers(drv))
            if dl.empty:
                continue
            times = dl["LapTime"].dropna()
            if len(times) == 0:
                continue
            pace_data.append(
                {
                    "driver": str(drv),
                    "team": str(dl.iloc[0]["Team"]),
                    "avgLapTime": round(times.mean().total_seconds(), 3),
                    "medianLapTime": round(times.median().total_seconds(), 3),
                    "fastestLap": round(times.min().total_seconds(), 3),
                    "laps": len(times),
                }
            )

        pace_data.sort(key=lambda x: x["avgLapTime"])
        return {"year": year, "race": race, "pace": pace_data}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@router.get("/season-progression/{era_key}")
def season_progression(era_key: str):
    """Compatibility endpoint; web UI uses the progress-reporting job instead."""
    return _season_progression(era_key)


@router.post("/season-jobs/{era_key}")
def start_season(era_key: str):
    from ..services import jobs

    if era_key not in ERAS:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Era not found")
    try:
        return jobs.start(
            era_key, lambda progress: _season_progression(era_key, progress)
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(429, str(exc)) from exc


@router.get("/season-jobs/status/{job_id}")
def season_status(job_id: str):
    from ..services import jobs

    result = jobs.get(job_id)
    if result is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            404, "Analysis job expired or server restarted; reload the page"
        )
    return result

"""
Ghost Car API routes.
Provides telemetry data, track layouts, and driver comparison data
for the React frontend to render interactive visualizations.
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import fastf1
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Optional, List
from pathlib import Path

router = APIRouter()

from ..services.data import load_session, CACHE_DIR, optional_data, number
from ..services.openf1 import TELEMETRY_NOTE


def _provenance(session):
    warnings = list(getattr(session, "data_warnings", []))
    if getattr(session, "data_provider", None) == "openf1":
        warnings.append(TELEMETRY_NOTE)
    return {
        "dataSource": getattr(session, "data_source", "FastF1"),
        "warnings": warnings,
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers – reuse data_loader logic without importing the CLI module
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _load_session(year, round_number, session_type):
    return load_session(year, round_number, session_type, telemetry=True)


def _telemetry_to_dict(telemetry) -> dict:
    """Convert FastF1 telemetry DataFrame to JSON-safe dict of arrays."""
    required = [
        "X",
        "Y",
        "Speed",
        "Throttle",
        "Brake",
        "nGear",
        "Distance",
        "Time",
    ]
    telemetry = (
        telemetry.dropna(subset=required).sort_values("Time").drop_duplicates("Time")
    )
    if len(telemetry) < 2:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Not enough finite telemetry samples")
    result = {
        key: telemetry[column].astype(float).tolist()
        for key, column in {
            "x": "X",
            "y": "Y",
            "speed": "Speed",
            "throttle": "Throttle",
            "brake": "Brake",
            "gear": "nGear",
            "distance": "Distance",
        }.items()
    }
    result["drs"] = [
        number(v)
        for v in telemetry.get("DRS", pd.Series(np.nan, index=telemetry.index))
    ]
    result["time"] = telemetry["Time"].dt.total_seconds().tolist()
    if not all(
        v is None or np.isfinite(v) for values in result.values() for v in values
    ):
        raise HTTPException(404, "Telemetry contains non-finite values")
    return result


def _format_lap_time(td) -> str:
    total = td.total_seconds()
    mins = int(total // 60)
    secs = total % 60
    return f"{mins}:{secs:06.3f}"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Endpoints
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


@router.get("/track/{year}/{round_number}/{session_type}")
def get_track_layout(year: int, round_number: int, session_type: str):
    """Return track X/Y coordinates from the fastest lap."""
    try:
        session = _load_session(year, round_number, session_type)
        fastest = session.laps.pick_fastest()
        if fastest is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="No fastest lap found")
        tel = fastest.get_telemetry()
        if tel is None or tel.empty:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="No telemetry data")

        circuit_info = {}
        try:
            ci = session.get_circuit_info()
            circuit_info = {
                "rotation": float(ci.rotation) if hasattr(ci, "rotation") else 0.0,
                "corners": (
                    ci.corners.to_dict("records") if hasattr(ci, "corners") else []
                ),
            }
        except Exception:
            pass

        return {
            **_provenance(session),
            "track": {"x": tel["X"].tolist(), "y": tel["Y"].tolist()},
            "circuitInfo": circuit_info,
            "eventName": str(session.event.get("EventName", "")),
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@router.get("/fastest-lap/{year}/{round_number}/{session_type}/{driver}")
def get_fastest_lap(year: int, round_number: int, session_type: str, driver: str):
    """Return telemetry for a driver's fastest lap."""
    try:
        session = _load_session(year, round_number, session_type)
        laps = session.laps.pick_drivers(driver)
        fastest = laps.pick_fastest()
        if fastest is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"No fastest lap for {driver}")

        tel = fastest.get_telemetry()
        if tel is None or tel.empty:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="No telemetry")

        lap_time = fastest["LapTime"]
        return {
            **_provenance(session),
            "driver": driver,
            "team": str(fastest.get("Team", "Unknown")),
            "lapTime": _format_lap_time(lap_time),
            "lapTimeSeconds": lap_time.total_seconds(),
            "compound": str(fastest.get("Compound", "Unknown")),
            "telemetry": _telemetry_to_dict(tel),
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@router.get("/compare/{year}/{round_number}/{session_type}/{driver1}/{driver2}")
def compare_drivers(
    year: int, round_number: int, session_type: str, driver1: str, driver2: str
):
    """Compare fastest laps of two drivers – returns telemetry for both."""
    try:
        session = _load_session(year, round_number, session_type)

        def _get_lap(drv):
            laps = session.laps.pick_drivers(drv)
            fastest = laps.pick_fastest()
            if fastest is None:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"No lap for {drv}")
            tel = fastest.get_telemetry()
            if tel is None or tel.empty:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"No telemetry for {drv}")
            return fastest, tel

        lap1, tel1 = _get_lap(driver1)
        lap2, tel2 = _get_lap(driver2)

        # Sanitize before interpolation as well as before serialization.
        clean1, clean2 = _telemetry_to_dict(tel1), _telemetry_to_dict(tel2)
        max_dist = min(max(clean1["distance"]), max(clean2["distance"]))
        dist_pts = np.linspace(
            max(min(clean1["distance"]), min(clean2["distance"])), max_dist, 500
        ).tolist()
        comparison = {"distance": dist_pts}
        for index, clean in enumerate((clean1, clean2), 1):
            order = np.argsort(clean["distance"])
            dist = np.asarray(clean["distance"])[order]
            for channel in ("speed", "throttle", "brake", "gear", "drs", "time"):
                values = np.asarray(clean[channel])[order]
                if channel == "time":
                    values = values - values[0]
                if channel in ("brake", "gear", "drs"):
                    indices = np.clip(
                        np.searchsorted(dist, dist_pts, side="right") - 1,
                        0,
                        len(dist) - 1,
                    )
                    aligned = values[indices].tolist()
                else:
                    aligned = np.interp(dist_pts, dist, values).tolist()
                comparison[f"{channel}{index}"] = aligned
        comparison["speedDelta"] = [
            round(a - b, 3) for a, b in zip(comparison["speed1"], comparison["speed2"])
        ]
        comparison["timeDelta"] = [
            round(a - b, 4) for a, b in zip(comparison["time1"], comparison["time2"])
        ]

        return {
            **_provenance(session),
            "eventName": str(session.event.get("EventName", "")),
            "driver1": {
                "abbreviation": driver1,
                "team": str(lap1.get("Team", "")),
                "lapTime": _format_lap_time(lap1["LapTime"]),
                "lapTimeSeconds": lap1["LapTime"].total_seconds(),
                "compound": str(lap1.get("Compound", "")),
                "telemetry": clean1,
            },
            "driver2": {
                "abbreviation": driver2,
                "team": str(lap2.get("Team", "")),
                "lapTime": _format_lap_time(lap2["LapTime"]),
                "lapTimeSeconds": lap2["LapTime"].total_seconds(),
                "compound": str(lap2.get("Compound", "")),
                "telemetry": clean2,
            },
            "comparison": comparison,
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@router.get("/weather/{year}/{round_number}/{session_type}")
def get_weather(year: int, round_number: int, session_type: str):
    """Return session weather data."""
    try:
        session = load_session(year, round_number, session_type)
        wd = optional_data(session, "weather_data")
        if wd is None or wd.empty:
            return {"weather": None}
        return {
            "weather": {
                "airTemp": number(wd["AirTemp"].mean(), 1),
                "trackTemp": number(wd["TrackTemp"].mean(), 1),
                "humidity": number(wd["Humidity"].mean(), 1),
                "windSpeed": number(wd["WindSpeed"].mean(), 1),
                "rainfall": bool(wd["Rainfall"].dropna().gt(0).any()),
            }
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

FILE: ./backend/routers/prediction.py

"""Model adapters, immutable forecasts, and post-race scoring."""

import importlib.util
import inspect
import hashlib
import logging
import threading
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from ..services.data import ROOT, finished, iso, load_session, number, utc_now
from ..services.loading_errors import SessionDataError
from ..services.empirical_model import artifacts, features, infer
from ..services import prediction_store as store
from .common import get_schedule

router = APIRouter()
PREDICTION_DIR = ROOT / "formula1-prediction"
SIMULATION_LOCK = threading.Lock()
CHANGES = {
    1: "Original long-run regression, fuel assumptions and Monte Carlo framework",
    2: "More simulations and revised stint detection and traffic penalties",
    3: "Revised grid fallbacks and stint assumptions",
    4: "Track-dependent overtaking constraints and race distance",
    5: "Revised traffic and race simulation heuristics",
    6: "Further safety-car and racecraft heuristics",
    7: "Additional pace anomaly and driver adjustments",
    8: "Revised driver and pace adjustments",
    9: "Further anomaly handling and driver-tier assumptions",
    10: "Qualifying-anchored pace, replacing the long-run physics dependency",
    11: "Grid position and track-dependent traffic drag",
    12: "22-car field and winning-margin simulation; hardcoded 2026 driver tiers",
}


def _discover_versions():
    paths = {1: PREDICTION_DIR / "f1_predictor.py"}
    for path in PREDICTION_DIR.glob("f1_predictor_v*.py"):
        tag = path.stem.removeprefix("f1_predictor_v")
        if tag.isdigit():
            paths[int(tag)] = path
    return {f"v{v}": path for v, path in sorted(paths.items()) if path.exists()}


def _load_module(path):
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(path.stem, path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


@router.get("/models")
def list_models():
    models = [
        {
            "id": key,
            "name": f"{key} · Legacy simulation",
            "improvements": [CHANGES.get(int(key[1:]), "Legacy iteration")],
            "limitations": [
                "Heuristic assumptions; no demonstrated calibration or validated improvement over prior versions"
            ],
            "sessions": ["Q"] if key in ("v10", "v11") else ["FP1", "FP2", "FP3", "Q"],
            "metrics": None,
            "trainedThrough": None,
        }
        for key in _discover_versions()
    ]
    for artifact in artifacts():
        models.append(
            {
                key: artifact.get(key)
                for key in (
                    "id",
                    "name",
                    "improvements",
                    "limitations",
                    "metrics",
                    "trainedThrough",
                    "trainingEvents",
                    "baselines",
                    "improvesGridLogLoss",
                    "previousComparable",
                    "heldoutEventKeys",
                )
            }
        )
        models[-1]["sessions"] = ["Q"]
    return {
        "models": [m["id"] for m in models],
        "catalog": models,
        "defaultModel": models[-1]["id"] if models else None,
        "trainingCommand": "python -m backend.train_predictor --from-year 2023 --through-year 2025",
    }


class PredictionRequest(BaseModel):
    year: int = Field(2026, ge=2018, le=2100)
    gp: str = Field("Great Britain", min_length=1, max_length=100)
    session: Literal["FP1", "FP2", "FP3", "Q"] = "Q"
    model: str = Field("v12", max_length=100)
    seed: int = Field(17, ge=0, le=2**32 - 1)


def grid_data(session, kind):
    if kind != "Q":
        from ..services.data import clean_laps

        laps = clean_laps(session.laps)
        if laps.empty:
            raise ValueError("No valid practice laps")
        best = laps.loc[
            laps.groupby("Driver")["LapTime"].idxmin(), ["Driver", "LapTime"]
        ]
        best = (
            best.rename(columns={"Driver": "Abbreviation"})
            .sort_values("LapTime")
            .reset_index(drop=True)
        )
        best["GridPosition"] = np.arange(1, len(best) + 1)
        return best
    return session.results.sort_values("Position").copy()


def normalize_counts(raw):
    counts, margins = raw if isinstance(raw, tuple) else (raw, None)
    total = float(sum(counts.values()))
    if not counts or not np.isfinite(total) or total <= 0:
        raise ValueError("Model returned no finite winner distribution")
    if any(not np.isfinite(v) or v < 0 for v in counts.values()):
        raise ValueError("Model returned invalid winner counts")
    # v1 has a weight-only fallback: normalize actual output, never divide it by configured runs.
    return {str(d): float(w) / total for d, w in counts.items()}, margins, total


def legacy(req, session):
    model = req.model.lower()
    if model == "v1 (original – physics + ml)":
        model = "v1"
    paths = _discover_versions()
    if model not in paths:
        raise ValueError("Unknown model; select an exact ID from the catalog")
    if model in ("v10", "v11") and req.session != "Q":
        raise ValueError("This qualifying-only model cannot use a practice session")
    module = _load_module(paths[model])
    frame = grid_data(session, req.session)
    grid = frame["Abbreviation"].dropna().tolist()
    profile, warnings = {}, list(getattr(session, "data_warnings", [])) + [
        "Legacy simulation uses manually configured assumptions and may contain driver-specific bonuses. These are not empirical skill estimates."
    ]
    params = inspect.signature(module.RaceSimulator.__init__).parameters
    # UI event names differ from legacy dictionary keys (e.g. British GP / Great Britain).
    aliases = {
        "British Grand Prix": "Great Britain",
        "Australian Grand Prix": "Australia",
        "Italian Grand Prix": "Italy",
        "Japanese Grand Prix": "Japan",
        "Belgian Grand Prix": "Belgium",
        "Spanish Grand Prix": "Spain",
        "Canadian Grand Prix": "Canada",
        "Austrian Grand Prix": "Austria",
        "Hungarian Grand Prix": "Hungary",
        "Dutch Grand Prix": "Netherlands",
        "Brazilian Grand Prix": "Brazil",
        "São Paulo Grand Prix": "Brazil",
        "Mexico City Grand Prix": "Mexico",
        "United States Grand Prix": "United States",
    }
    gp_name = aliases.get(req.gp, req.gp.removesuffix(" Grand Prix"))
    with SIMULATION_LOCK:
        # Legacy modules use NumPy's global generator. Restore it after every request.
        before = np.random.get_state()
        try:
            np.random.seed(req.seed)
            if "physics_profile" in params:
                practice = session if req.session != "Q" else None
                if practice is None:
                    for kind in ("FP3", "FP2", "FP1"):
                        try:
                            candidate = session.event.get_session(kind)
                            if pd.Timestamp(candidate.date) < pd.Timestamp(
                                session.date
                            ):
                                candidate = load_session(req.year, req.gp, kind)
                                if finished(candidate):
                                    practice = candidate
                                    break
                        except Exception:
                            continue
                if practice is not None:
                    warnings.extend(getattr(practice, "data_warnings", []))
                    analyzer = module.TelemetryAnalyzer.__new__(
                        module.TelemetryAnalyzer
                    )
                    profile = analyzer.analyze_stints(practice)
                    # Original v1 keys profiles by driver number; simulation uses abbreviations.
                    profile = {
                        (
                            str(practice.get_driver(k)["Abbreviation"])
                            if str(k).isdigit()
                            else str(k)
                        ): v
                        for k, v in profile.items()
                    }
                if not profile:
                    warnings.append(
                        "No usable pre-cutoff long runs: grid-based fallback, not a measured race-pace estimate"
                    )
                kwargs = {"gp_name": gp_name} if "gp_name" in params else {"laps": 58}
                # Do not load the target race to discover laps (would leak future information).
                if "laps" in kwargs:
                    kwargs = {"laps": 58}
                    warnings.append("Legacy v1–v3 race distance defaults to 58 laps")
                sim = module.RaceSimulator(profile, grid, **kwargs)
            else:
                sim = module.RaceSimulator(frame, gp_name)
            probs, margins, total = normalize_counts(sim.run_simulation())
        finally:
            np.random.set_state(before)
    return probs, margins, total, warnings, model


@router.post("/run")
def run_prediction(req: PredictionRequest):
    try:
        session = load_session(
            req.year, req.gp, req.session, results_only=req.session == "Q"
        )
        if not finished(session):
            raise ValueError(
                "Prediction input session has not finished; timing may still be provisional"
            )
        cutoff, trained_through, margins = iso(session.date), None, None
        inputs = {
            "sessionStart": cutoff,
            "dataSource": getattr(session, "data_source", "FastF1"),
        }
        source_hash = None
        if req.model.startswith("v13-"):
            artifact = next((a for a in artifacts() if a["id"] == req.model), None)
            if artifact is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Trained model not found; run the documented training command first"
                )
            if req.session != "Q":
                raise ValueError("Empirical v13 is trained for qualifying inputs only")
            session, entries, meta = features(req.year, req.gp)
            if pd.Timestamp(artifact["trainedThrough"]) >= pd.Timestamp(meta["cutoff"]):
                raise ValueError(
                    "This model was trained on results after the selected feature cutoff. Select an earlier-trained iteration."
                )
            inputs = {**meta, "entries": entries}
            source_hash = hashlib.sha256(
                __import__("json").dumps(artifact, sort_keys=True).encode()
            ).hexdigest()
            probs = dict(
                zip([e["driver"] for e in entries], infer(artifact, entries).tolist())
            )
            total, trained_through, model = (
                0,
                artifact["trainedThrough"],
                artifact["id"],
            )
            warnings = artifact["limitations"] + meta.get("dataWarnings", [])
        else:
            probs, margins, total, warnings, model = legacy(req, session)
            source_hash = hashlib.sha256(
                _discover_versions()[model].read_bytes()
            ).hexdigest()
        created = utc_now()
        race_start = iso(session.event.get("Session5DateUtc"))
        # Get race date by name: sprint weekends need not use a fixed session slot.
        for index in range(1, 6):
            if session.event.get(f"Session{index}") == "Race":
                race_start = iso(session.event.get(f"Session{index}DateUtc"))
        mode = (
            "pre-race"
            if race_start and pd.Timestamp(created) < pd.Timestamp(race_start)
            else "retrospective"
        )
        order = sorted(probs, key=probs.get, reverse=True)
        return store.save(
            {
                "year": req.year,
                "gp": str(session.event["EventName"]),
                "round": int(session.event["RoundNumber"]),
                "session": req.session,
                "sessionLabel": (
                    "Qualifying"
                    if req.session == "Q"
                    else f"{req.session} · provisional grid"
                ),
                "model": model,
                "seed": req.seed,
                "monteCarloRuns": int(total),
                "createdAt": created,
                "featureCutoff": cutoff,
                "trainedThrough": trained_through,
                "mode": mode,
                "warnings": warnings,
                "probabilities": probs,
                "modelSourceHash": source_hash,
                "inputs": inputs,
                "winningMargins": (
                    {
                        k: number(v / sum(margins.values()) * 100)
                        for k, v in margins.items()
                    }
                    if margins
                    else None
                ),
                "predictions": [
                    {
                        "position": i + 1,
                        "driver": d,
                        "winProbability": number(probs[d] * 100, 3),
                    }
                    for i, d in enumerate(order)
                ],
            }
        )
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(422, str(exc)) from exc
    except Exception as exc:
        logging.exception("Prediction execution failed")
        raise HTTPException(
            502,
            "Prediction failed while loading data or running the model. Check the backend log.",
        ) from exc


@router.get("/history")
def history():
    return {
        "runs": [
            {
                k: r.get(k)
                for k in ("runId", "year", "gp", "round", "model", "mode", "createdAt")
            }
            for r in store.recent()
        ]
    }


@router.get("/evaluate/{run_id}")
def evaluate(run_id: str):
    run = store.get(run_id)
    if run is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Prediction snapshot not found")
    try:
        race = load_session(run["year"], run["round"], "R", results_only=True)
        if not finished(race):
            return {
                "status": "pending",
                "detail": "Race not finished or results not published yet",
            }
        results = race.results.dropna(subset=["Position"]).sort_values("Position")
        if results.empty or float(results.iloc[0]["Position"]) != 1:
            return {"status": "pending", "detail": "Winner classification unavailable"}
        winner = str(results.iloc[0]["Abbreviation"])
        drivers = set(run["probabilities"]) | set(results["Abbreviation"].astype(str))
        p = run["probabilities"]
        brier = sum((p.get(d, 0) - int(d == winner)) ** 2 for d in drivers)
        leader = max(p, key=p.get)
        return {
            "status": "scored",
            "mode": run["mode"],
            "winner": winner,
            "predictedWinner": leader,
            "winnerProbability": number(p.get(winner, 0) * 100),
            "correct": leader == winner,
            "brier": number(brier),
            "logLoss": number(-np.log(max(p.get(winner, 0), 1e-15))),
            "asOf": utc_now(),
            "note": "Winner-distribution score, not finishing-order accuracy. Lower Brier/log loss is better. Results may change after penalties.",
            "results": [
                {
                    "position": int(r["Position"]),
                    "driver": str(r["Abbreviation"]),
                    "predictedProbability": number(
                        p.get(str(r["Abbreviation"]), 0) * 100
                    ),
                }
                for _, r in results.iterrows()
            ],
        }
    except SessionDataError as exc:
        if exc.status_code == 425:
            return {"status": "pending", "detail": str(exc.detail)}
        raise
    except Exception:
        return {
            "status": "pending",
            "detail": "Race results not available from provider; retry later",
        }


@router.get("/grand-prix/{year}")
def list_grand_prix(year: int):
    result = get_schedule(year)
    result["events"] = [e for e in result["events"] if not e["isTesting"]]
    return result


============================================================
FILE: ./backend/routers/standings.py
============================================================
"""Championship snapshots from Jolpica through FastF1's maintained interface."""

import time
from functools import lru_cache

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Query
from fastf1.ergast import Ergast

from ..services.data import number, utc_now

router = APIRouter()


def rows(response):
    if not response.content:
        return [], None
    return response.content[0].to_dict("records"), int(
        response.description.iloc[0]["round"]
    )


def driver_row(row):
    teams = row.get("constructorNames", [])
    return {
        "id": row["driverId"],
        "label": row.get("driverCode") or row["familyName"],
        "name": f'{row["givenName"]} {row["familyName"]}',
        "team": " / ".join(teams) if isinstance(teams, list) else str(teams),
        "position": int(row["position"]),
        "points": number(row["points"]),
        "wins": int(row["wins"]),
    }


def constructor_row(row):
    return {
        "id": row["constructorId"],
        "label": row["constructorName"],
        "name": row["constructorName"],
        "team": row["constructorName"],
        "position": int(row["position"]),
        "points": number(row["points"]),
        "wins": int(row["wins"]),
    }


def changes(current, previous):
    lookup = {r["id"]: r for r in previous}
    for row in current:
        before = lookup.get(row["id"])
        row.update(
            previousPosition=before["position"] if before else None,
            previousPoints=before["points"] if before else None,
            positionChange=before["position"] - row["position"] if before else None,
            pointsChange=number(row["points"] - before["points"]) if before else None,
        )
    return sorted(current, key=lambda r: r["position"])


@lru_cache(maxsize=24)
def snapshot(year, requested_round, bucket):
    client = Ergast()
    raw, actual_round = rows(
        client.get_driver_standings(
            season=year, round=requested_round or "last", limit=100
        )
    )
    if actual_round is None:
        return {
            "year": year,
            "round": None,
            "previousRound": None,
            "drivers": [],
            "constructors": [],
            "asOf": utc_now(),
            "source": "Jolpica-F1 via FastF1",
        }
    cons, cons_round = rows(
        client.get_constructor_standings(season=year, round=actual_round, limit=100)
    )
    if cons_round != actual_round:
        raise ValueError(
            "Driver and constructor standings are not published for the same round yet"
        )
    prev_d, prev_c = [], []
    if actual_round > 1:
        prev_d, prev_round = rows(
            client.get_driver_standings(season=year, round=actual_round - 1, limit=100)
        )
        prev_c, prev_c_round = rows(
            client.get_constructor_standings(
                season=year, round=actual_round - 1, limit=100
            )
        )
        if prev_round != actual_round - 1 or prev_c_round != actual_round - 1:
            raise ValueError(
                "Previous-round standings unavailable; change cannot be calculated reliably"
            )
    return {
        "year": year,
        "round": actual_round,
        "previousRound": actual_round - 1 if actual_round > 1 else None,
        "drivers": changes(
            [driver_row(r) for r in raw], [driver_row(r) for r in prev_d]
        ),
        "constructors": changes(
            [constructor_row(r) for r in cons], [constructor_row(r) for r in prev_c]
        ),
        "asOf": utc_now(),
        "source": "Jolpica-F1 via FastF1",
        "comparison": "Previous completed Grand Prix round; sprint points and published adjustments included.",
    }


@router.get("/{year}")
def get_standings(year: int, round: int | None = Query(None, ge=1, le=40)):
    try:
        return snapshot(year, round, int(time.time() // 300))
    except Exception as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            502,
            "Championship data unavailable. Retry after the provider publishes the results.",
        ) from exc

FILE: ./backend/routers/calendar.py

"""Live F1 sessions plus source-labelled cross-series event calendars."""

import json
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path

import fastf1
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException

from ..services.data import iso, utc_now

router = APIRouter()


@lru_cache(maxsize=6)
def f1_calendar(year, bucket):
    from ..services.events import get_schedule

    schedule = get_schedule(year, include_testing=False)
    events = []
    for _, event in schedule.iterrows():
        for index in range(1, 6):
            name = event.get(f"Session{index}")
            start = iso(event.get(f"Session{index}DateUtc"))
            if not name or str(name) in ("None", "nan") or not start:
                continue
            events.append(
                {
                    "id": f'f1-{year}-{event["RoundNumber"]}-{index}',
                    "series": "f1",
                    "title": f'{event["EventName"]} — {name}',
                    "event": str(event["EventName"]),
                    "session": str(name),
                    "round": int(event["RoundNumber"]),
                    "start": start,
                    "end": None,
                    "dateOnly": False,
                    "location": str(event.get("Location", "")),
                    "source": "FastF1 event schedule",
                    "sourceUrl": "https://www.formula1.com/en/racing",
                    "verifiedAt": utc_now(),
                    "status": "published schedule",
                }
            )
    return events


@router.get("/f1/{year}")
def get_f1_calendar(year: int):
    try:
        events = f1_calendar(year, int(time.time() // 300))
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        future = [e for e in events if datetime.fromisoformat(e["start"]) > now]
        return {
            "events": events,
            "nextSession": min(future, key=lambda e: e["start"]) if future else None,
            "asOf": utc_now(),
            "year": year,
        }
    except Exception as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            502, "F1 session schedule is temporarily unavailable"
        ) from exc


@router.get("/motorsport/{year}")
def motorsport(year: int):
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "data" / f"motorsport-{year}.json"
    seed = (
        json.loads(path.read_text()) if path.exists() else {"events": [], "sources": []}
    )
    warnings = []
    try:
        events = f1_calendar(year, int(time.time() // 300)) + seed["events"]
    except Exception:
        events = seed["events"]
        warnings.append("Live F1 schedule unavailable; other series remain visible")
    return {
        "year": year,
        "events": sorted(events, key=lambda e: e["start"]),
        "sources": seed["sources"],
        "warnings": warnings,
        "note": "Other-series dates are a checked-in snapshot, not a live feed. Import a current .ics calendar in your browser for session times or later seasons. Date-only weekends stay in venue dates.",
    }

"""
Common API routes - seasons, schedules, drivers.
"""

import fastf1
import pandas as pd
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from pathlib import Path

router = APIRouter()

from ..services.data import load_session, CACHE_DIR
from ..services import events as public_events


@router.get("/seasons")
def get_seasons():
    """Return F1 seasons from 2018 through the current year."""
    return {
        "seasons": list(range(2018, __import__("datetime").datetime.now().year + 1))
    }


@router.get("/schedule/{year}")
def get_schedule(year: int):
    """Return full event schedule for a season."""
    try:
        schedule = public_events.get_schedule(year, include_testing=True)
        events = []
        testing_count = 0

        for _, row in schedule.iterrows():
            event_format = str(row.get("EventFormat", "")).lower()
            event_name = str(row.get("EventName", ""))

            if event_format == "testing" or "test" in event_name.lower():
                testing_count += 1
                events.append(
                    {
                        "roundNumber": 0,
                        "eventName": f"Pre-Season Testing {testing_count}",
                        "country": str(row.get("Country", "Unknown")),
                        "location": str(row.get("Location", "Unknown")),
                        "date": str(row.get("EventDate", ""))[:10],
                        "isTesting": True,
                        "testNumber": testing_count,
                    }
                )
                continue

            if pd.isna(row.get("RoundNumber")) or row.get("RoundNumber") == 0:
                continue

            events.append(
                {
                    "roundNumber": int(row["RoundNumber"]),
                    "eventName": event_name,
                    "country": str(row.get("Country", "Unknown")),
                    "location": str(row.get("Location", event_name)),
                    "date": str(row.get("EventDate", ""))[:10],
                    "isTesting": False,
                    "testNumber": 0,
                }
            )

        return {"year": year, "events": events}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@router.get("/sessions/{year}/{round_number}")
def get_sessions(year: int, round_number: int):
    """Return available session types for a given event."""
    try:
        event = public_events.get_event(year, round_number)
        sessions = []
        session_map = {
            "Practice 1": "FP1",
            "Practice 2": "FP2",
            "Practice 3": "FP3",
            "Qualifying": "Q",
            "Race": "R",
            "Sprint": "S",
            "Sprint Qualifying": "SQ",
            "Sprint Shootout": "SS",
        }
        for i in range(1, 6):
            session_name = event.get(f"Session{i}")
            if pd.notna(session_name) and session_name in session_map:
                sessions.append(
                    {
                        "key": session_map[session_name],
                        "name": session_name,
                    }
                )
        return {"sessions": sessions}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@router.get("/drivers/{year}/{round_number}/{session_type}")
def get_drivers(year: int, round_number: int, session_type: str):
    """Return drivers who participated in a session."""
    try:
        session = load_session(
            year,
            round_number,
            session_type,
            results_only=session_type in ("Q", "R", "S"),
        )
        drivers = []
        for _, row in session.results.iterrows():
            drivers.append(
                {
                    "abbreviation": str(row.get("Abbreviation", "")),
                    "fullName": str(row.get("FullName", "")),
                    "team": str(row.get("TeamName", "")),
                    "number": str(row.get("DriverNumber", "")),
                }
            )
        return {"drivers": drivers}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

"""Offline regression tests: no upstream downloads or private data required."""

import importlib.util
import json
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import patch

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from backend.app import app
from backend.routers import prediction, standings
from backend.services import empirical_model as ml, prediction_store as store
from backend.services.data import clean_laps
from backend.services.ical import parse_ics
from backend.services.weekend import (
    matched_laps,
    interval,
    degradation,
    qualifying_gap,
    field_pace,
)


def laps():
    rows = []
    for lap in range(1, 19):
        for driver, offset in [("AAA", 0.0), ("BBB", 0.4)]:
            rows.append(
                dict(
                    Driver=driver,
                    LapNumber=lap,
                    Compound="MEDIUM",
                    TyreLife=lap,
                    Stint=1,
                    LapTime=pd.Timedelta(seconds=80 + offset + lap * 0.01),
                    IsAccurate=True,
                    TrackStatus="1",
                    PitInTime=pd.NaT,
                    PitOutTime=pd.NaT,
                    Deleted=False,
                    FastF1Generated=False,
                )
            )
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def fake_events(count=30):
    result = []
    for i in range(count):
        entries = [
            {"driver": d, "values": [float(k), k / 3, k * 0.1, 0.0]}
            for k, d in enumerate(("AAA", "BBB", "CCC", "DDD"))
        ]
        result.append(
            {"key": str(i), "entries": entries, "winner": "AAA" if i % 4 else "BBB"}
        )
    return result


class Teammates(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_clean_excludes_pits_flags_deleted_and_synthesized(self):
        df = laps()
        df["PitInTime"] = pd.Series(pd.NaT, index=df.index, dtype="timedelta64[ns]")
        df.loc[0, "PitInTime"] = pd.Timedelta(seconds=1)
        df.loc[1, "TrackStatus"] = "14"
        df.loc[2, "Deleted"] = True
        df.loc[3, "FastF1Generated"] = True
        df.loc[4, "IsAccurate"] = False
        self.assertEqual(len(clean_laps(df)), len(df) - 5)

    def test_same_lap_compound_and_age_only(self):
        df = laps()
        df.loc[(df.Driver == "BBB") & (df.LapNumber == 2), "Compound"] = "HARD"
        df.loc[(df.Driver == "BBB") & (df.LapNumber == 3), "TyreLife"] = 20
        pairs = matched_laps(df, "AAA", "BBB")
        self.assertEqual(len(pairs), 16)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(pairs.delta.median(), -0.4)
        self.assertNotIn(2, pairs.LapNumber.tolist())
        self.assertNotIn(3, pairs.LapNumber.tolist())

    def test_interval_and_small_sample(self):
        pairs = matched_laps(laps(), "AAA", "BBB")
        self.assertIsNone(interval(pairs.head(7)))
        low, high = interval(pairs)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(low, -0.4)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(high, -0.4)

    def test_age_race_lap_collinearity_never_claims_wear(self):
        self.assertIsNone(degradation(laps(), "AAA")[0]["slopeSecondsPerTyreLap"])

    def test_field_pace_matches_compound_and_age_without_identity_bonuses(self):
        df = laps()
        third = df[df.Driver.eq("AAA")].copy()
        third["Driver"] = "CCC"
        third["LapTime"] += pd.Timedelta(seconds=0.8)
        df = pd.concat([df, third], ignore_index=True)
        results = pd.DataFrame(
            [
                {"Abbreviation": d, "TeamName": "Team", "Position": i + 1}
                for i, d in enumerate(["AAA", "BBB", "CCC"])
            ]
        )
        race = SimpleNamespace(laps=df, results=results)
        report = field_pace(race, SimpleNamespace(results=results))
        self.assertLess(report[0]["relativePacePercent"], 0)
        self.assertGreater(report[2]["relativePacePercent"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(report[0]["matchedLaps"], 18)
        self.assertIsNone(
            field_pace(SimpleNamespace(laps=laps(), results=results), None)[0][
                "relativePacePercent"
            ]
        )

    def test_qualifying_uses_shared_segment(self):
        q1 = laps()
        q2 = laps().query("Driver=='AAA'")
        q3 = q2
        session = SimpleNamespace(
            laps=SimpleNamespace(split_qualifying_sessions=lambda: [q1, q2, q3])
        )
        value = qualifying_gap(session, "AAA", "BBB")
        self.assertEqual(value["segment"], "Q1")
        self.assertAlmostEqual(value["deltaSeconds"], -0.4)


class Standings(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_movement_and_new_driver(self):
        current = [
            dict(id="a", position=1, points=80),
            dict(id="new", position=2, points=9),
        ]
        previous = [dict(id="a", position=3, points=55)]
        result = standings.changes(current, previous)
        self.assertEqual(result[0]["positionChange"], 2)
        self.assertEqual(result[0]["pointsChange"], 25)
        self.assertIsNone(result[1]["positionChange"])
        self.assertIsNone(result[1]["pointsChange"])

    def test_same_round_for_both_championships(self):
        def response(data, rd):
            return SimpleNamespace(
                content=[pd.DataFrame(data)], description=pd.DataFrame([{"round": rd}])
            )

        driver = dict(
            driverId="a",
            driverCode="AAA",
            givenName="A",
            familyName="Driver",
            constructorNames=["Team"],
            position=1,
            points=100,
            wins=1,
        )
        cons = dict(
            constructorId="t", constructorName="Team", position=1, points=100, wins=1
        )
        client = SimpleNamespace(
            get_driver_standings=lambda **kw: response(
                [driver], 3 if kw["round"] == "last" else kw["round"]
            ),
            get_constructor_standings=lambda **kw: response([cons], kw["round"]),
        )
        standings.snapshot.cache_clear()
        with patch.object(standings, "Ergast", return_value=client):
            result = standings.snapshot(2025, None, 0)
        self.assertEqual(result["round"], 3)
        self.assertEqual(result["previousRound"], 2)
        self.assertEqual(result["constructors"][0]["pointsChange"], 0)
        standings.snapshot.cache_clear()


class Models(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_numeric_version_order_and_exact_ids(self):
        self.assertEqual(
            list(prediction._discover_versions()), [f"v{i}" for i in range(1, 13)]
        )

    def test_v1_weights_are_normalized(self):
        probs, _, _ = prediction.normalize_counts({"AAA": 50, "BBB": 30, "CCC": 10})
        self.assertAlmostEqual(sum(probs.values()), 1.0)

    def test_v12_tuple(self):
        probs, margins, _ = prediction.normalize_counts(
            ({"AAA": 8, "BBB": 2}, {"0-5": 10})
        )
        self.assertEqual(probs["AAA"], 0.8)
        self.assertEqual(margins, {"0-5": 10})

    def test_invalid_distribution_rejected(self):
        for value in ({"AAA": 0}, {"AAA": np.nan}, {"AAA": -1, "BBB": 3}):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                prediction.normalize_counts(value)

    def test_timedelta_fix_all_quali_models(self):
        frame = pd.DataFrame({"Abbreviation": ["AAA"], "Q3": pd.to_timedelta(["80s"])})
        for key in ("v10", "v11", "v12"):
            module = prediction._load_module(prediction._discover_versions()[key])
            sim = module.RaceSimulator(frame, "Bahrain")
            self.assertEqual(sim.get_quali_pace("AAA"), 80.0)

    def test_every_legacy_constructor_and_output(self):
        frame = pd.DataFrame(
            {
                "Abbreviation": ["AAA", "BBB"],
                "Q1": pd.to_timedelta(["80s", "81s"]),
                "Q2": pd.to_timedelta(["80s", "81s"]),
                "Q3": pd.to_timedelta(["80s", "81s"]),
                "GridPosition": [1, 2],
                "Position": [1, 2],
            }
        )
        for model, path in prediction._discover_versions().items():
            module = prediction._load_module(path)
            module.Config.MONTE_CARLO_RUNS = 5
            session = SimpleNamespace(
                results=frame,
                event=SimpleNamespace(
                    get_session=lambda _: (_ for _ in ()).throw(
                        ValueError("no practice")
                    )
                ),
            )
            with patch.object(prediction, "_load_module", return_value=module):
                probs, _, _, _, _ = prediction.legacy(
                    prediction.PredictionRequest(year=2025, gp="Bahrain", model=model),
                    session,
                )
            self.assertAlmostEqual(sum(probs.values()), 1.0, msg=model)
            self.assertEqual(set(probs), {"AAA", "BBB"})

    def test_empirical_model_has_no_names_as_features_and_sums_to_one(self):
        model = ml.fit(fake_events(), 1.0)
        self.assertFalse(
            any(
                "driver" in f.lower() or "team" in f.lower()
                for f in model["featureNames"]
            )
        )
        p = ml.infer(model, fake_events()[0]["entries"])
        self.assertAlmostEqual(float(p.sum()), 1.0)
        self.assertTrue(np.isfinite(p).all())

    def test_scores_penalize_confident_wrong_winner(self):
        event = fake_events(1)
        right = ml.metrics(event, [np.array([0.1, 0.7, 0.1, 0.1])])
        wrong = ml.metrics(event, [np.array([0.97, 0.01, 0.01, 0.01])])
        self.assertLess(right["logLoss"], wrong["logLoss"])
        self.assertLess(right["brier"], wrong["brier"])

    def test_future_training_cutoff_rejected_before_inference(self):
        client = TestClient(app)
        session = SimpleNamespace(date=pd.Timestamp("2025-03-01"))
        with patch.object(
            prediction, "load_session", return_value=session
        ), patch.object(prediction, "finished", return_value=True), patch.object(
            prediction,
            "features",
            return_value=(session, [], {"cutoff": "2025-03-01T00:00:00+00:00"}),
        ), patch.object(
            prediction,
            "artifacts",
            return_value=[
                {"id": "v13-test", "trainedThrough": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00"}
            ],
        ):
            res = client.post(
                "/api/prediction/run",
                json={"year": 2025, "gp": "Bahrain", "model": "v13-test"},
            )
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 422)
        self.assertIn("after the selected", res.json()["detail"])

    def test_training_has_disjoint_chronological_holdout_and_new_manifest(self):
        schedule = pd.DataFrame(
            [
                {
                    "RoundNumber": i,
                    "EventDate": pd.Timestamp("2024-01-01") + pd.Timedelta(days=i * 7),
                }
                for i in range(1, 21)
            ]
        )

        def feature_stub(year, rd, kind):
            event = fake_events(20)[rd - 1]
            date = pd.Timestamp("2024-01-01", tz="UTC") + pd.Timedelta(days=rd * 7)
            return None, event["entries"], {"cutoff": date.isoformat()}

        def race_stub(year, rd, kind, **kwargs):
            event = fake_events(20)[rd - 1]
            date = pd.Timestamp("2024-01-01", tz="UTC") + pd.Timedelta(
                days=rd * 7, hours=24
            )
            return SimpleNamespace(
                date=date,
                results=pd.DataFrame(
                    [{"Position": 1, "Abbreviation": event["winner"]}]
                ),
                laps=pd.DataFrame({"LapStartDate": [date]}),
            )

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp, patch.object(
            ml, "MODEL_DIR", Path(temp)
        ), patch.object(ml, "features", side_effect=feature_stub), patch.object(
            ml, "load_session", side_effect=race_stub
        ), patch.object(
            ml, "finished", return_value=True
        ), patch(
            "fastf1.get_event_schedule", return_value=schedule
        ):
            result = ml.train(2024, 2024)
            self.assertEqual(
                result["heldoutEventKeys"], ["2024-17", "2024-18", "2024-19", "2024-20"]
            )
            self.assertEqual(result["trainingEvents"], 20)
            self.assertEqual(result["metrics"]["events"], 4)
            self.assertEqual(result["baselines"]["uniform"]["events"], 4)
            self.assertEqual(len(list(Path(temp).glob("v13-*.json"))), 1)
            again = ml.train(2024, 2024)
            self.assertEqual(result["id"], again["id"])


class PersistenceAndEvaluation(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_snapshots_survive_new_connections_and_are_immutable(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp, patch.object(
            store, "DATA_DIR", Path(temp)
        ):
            original = {"createdAt": "2025-01-01", "probabilities": {"AAA": 1.0}}
            saved = store.save(original)
            original["probabilities"]["AAA"] = 0.0
            self.assertEqual(store.get(saved["runId"])["probabilities"]["AAA"], 1.0)
            self.assertEqual(len(store.recent()), 1)

    def test_evaluation_scores_saved_probabilities(self):
        run = {
            "year": 2025,
            "round": 1,
            "mode": "pre-race",
            "probabilities": {"AAA": 0.8, "BBB": 0.2},
        }
        race = SimpleNamespace(
            results=pd.DataFrame(
                [
                    {"Position": 1, "Abbreviation": "BBB"},
                    {"Position": 2, "Abbreviation": "AAA"},
                ]
            )
        )
        with patch.object(store, "get", return_value=run), patch.object(
            prediction, "load_session", return_value=race
        ), patch.object(prediction, "finished", return_value=True):
            result = prediction.evaluate("id")
        self.assertFalse(result["correct"])
        self.assertAlmostEqual(result["brier"], 1.28)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(result["logLoss"], 1.6094, places=4)

    def test_unfinished_race_not_scored(self):
        with patch.object(
            store, "get", return_value={"year": 2025, "round": 1}
        ), patch.object(
            prediction, "load_session", return_value=object()
        ), patch.object(
            prediction, "finished", return_value=False
        ):
            self.assertEqual(prediction.evaluate("id")["status"], "pending")


class Calendars(unittest.TestCase):
    def parse(self, body):
        return parse_ics(
            "BEGIN:VCALENDAR\nVERSION:2.0\nBEGIN:VEVENT\nUID:test\nSUMMARY:Race\n"
            + body
            + "\nEND:VEVENT\nEND:VCALENDAR",
            "wec",
            "https://example.com",
            "2026-11-07",
        )

    def test_utc_session(self):
        row = self.parse("DTSTART:20261108T100000Z")[0]
        self.assertEqual(row["start"], "2026-11-08T10:00:00+00:00")
        self.assertFalse(row["dateOnly"])

    def test_dst_conversion(self):
        row = self.parse("DTSTART;TZID=Australia/Sydney:20261011T110000")[0]
        self.assertEqual(row["start"], "2026-10-11T00:00:00+00:00")

    def test_date_only_not_shifted(self):
        row = self.parse("DTSTART;VALUE=DATE:20261106\nDTEND;VALUE=DATE:20261109")[0]
        self.assertEqual(row["start"], "2026-11-06")
        self.assertEqual(row["end"], "2026-11-09")
        self.assertTrue(row["dateOnly"])

    def test_floating_and_recurring_rejected(self):
        for text in (
            "DTSTART:20261108T100000",
            "DTSTART:20261108T100000Z\nRRULE:FREQ=DAILY",
        ):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                self.parse(text)

    def test_cancelled_event_omitted(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.parse("DTSTART:20261108T100000Z\nSTATUS:CANCELLED"), [])

    def test_shipped_snapshots_have_explicit_dates_and_https_sources(self):
        path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "data" / "motorsport-2026.json"
        data = json.loads(path.read_text())
        self.assertEqual(len({e["id"] for e in data["events"]}), len(data["events"]))
        for event in data["events"]:
            self.assertTrue(event["sourceUrl"].startswith("https://"))
            if not event["dateOnly"]:
                self.assertIsNotNone(pd.Timestamp(event["start"]).tzinfo)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

"""Regression coverage for partial FastF1 loads and October 2026 archive auth."""

import tempfile
import threading
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch

import fastf1
import pandas as pd
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from backend.app import app
from backend.services import data, f1_access, results
from backend.services.loading_errors import SessionDataError
from backend.services import weekend as analysis
from backend.routers import prediction


def template():
    return SimpleNamespace(
        date=pd.Timestamp("2026-10-31"),
        name="Qualifying",
        api_path="/static/2026/test/qualifying/",
        event={"RoundNumber": 19, "EventName": "Mexico City Grand Prix"},
    )


def lap_session():
    return SimpleNamespace(
        laps=pd.DataFrame({"LapTime": pd.to_timedelta(["80s", "81s"])}),
        weather_data=pd.DataFrame(),
        race_control_messages=pd.DataFrame(),
    )


def public_response():
    frame = pd.DataFrame(
        [
            dict(
                number=1,
                driverCode="AAA",
                givenName="A",
                familyName="Driver",
                constructorName="Team",
                position=1,
                Q1=pd.Timedelta(seconds=80),
                Q2=pd.NaT,
                Q3=pd.NaT,
            ),
            dict(
                number=2,
                driverCode="BBB",
                givenName="B",
                familyName="Driver",
                constructorName="Team",
                position=2,
                Q1=pd.Timedelta(seconds=80.2),
                Q2=pd.NaT,
                Q3=pd.NaT,
            ),
        ]
    )
    return SimpleNamespace(content=[frame])


class Loader(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        provider = patch.dict("os.environ", {"F1_TIMING_PROVIDER": "fastf1"})
        provider.start()
        self.addCleanup(provider.stop)
        data._session.cache_clear()
        data._FAILURES.clear()
        cache_probe = patch.object(f1_access, "has_cached_timing", return_value=True)
        cache_probe.start()
        self.addCleanup(cache_probe.stop)

    def test_old_fastf1_version_gives_upgrade_action(self):
        with patch.object(fastf1, "__version__", "3.7.0"):
            with self.assertRaises(SessionDataError) as caught:
                f1_access.check_version()
        self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, "fastf1_upgrade_required")

    def test_partial_driver_list_is_not_successful_timing(self):
        partial = SimpleNamespace(
            laps=pd.DataFrame({"LapTime": [pd.NaT]}),
            results=pd.DataFrame({"DriverNumber": range(22)}),
        )
        with patch.object(fastf1, "get_session", return_value=template()), patch.object(
            data, "_try_load"
        ), patch.object(
            f1_access,
            "ensure_authenticated",
            side_effect=SessionDataError("archive_auth_required", "Login required"),
        ):
            with self.assertRaises(SessionDataError) as caught:
                data._session(2026, 19, "Q", False, 1, None)
        self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, "archive_auth_required")
        self.assertEqual(data._session.cache_info().currsize, 0)
        self.assertFalse(data._valid(partial))

    def test_good_cache_load_does_not_require_auth(self):
        session = lap_session()
        session.date = pd.Timestamp("2025-01-01")
        with patch.object(fastf1, "get_session", return_value=session), patch.object(
            data, "_try_load"
        ), patch.object(f1_access, "ensure_authenticated") as auth:
            loaded = data._session(2025, 1, "Q", False, 1, None)
        auth.assert_not_called()
        self.assertEqual(len(loaded.laps), 2)
        self.assertEqual(len(loaded.data_warnings), 2)

    def test_network_load_is_validated_and_cached_only_after_success(self):
        first = template()
        second = lap_session()
        second.date = pd.Timestamp("2025-01-01")
        with patch.object(
            fastf1, "get_session", side_effect=[first, second]
        ), patch.object(data, "_try_load"), patch.object(
            f1_access, "ensure_authenticated"
        ) as auth, patch.object(
            f1_access, "probe_archive"
        ) as probe:
            loaded = data._session(2026, 19, "Q", False, 1, None)
        self.assertIs(loaded, second)
        auth.assert_called_once()
        probe.assert_called_once_with(second)

    def test_cold_cache_does_not_run_every_parser_before_auth_error(self):
        with patch.object(
            f1_access, "has_cached_timing", return_value=False
        ), patch.object(fastf1, "get_session", return_value=template()), patch.object(
            data, "_try_load"
        ) as parsers, patch.object(
            f1_access,
            "ensure_authenticated",
            side_effect=SessionDataError("archive_auth_required", "Login"),
        ):
            with self.assertRaises(SessionDataError):
                data._session(2026, 19, "Q", False, 1, None)
        parsers.assert_not_called()

    def test_missing_optional_weather_property_is_safe(self):
        class Session:
            @property
            def weather_data(self):
                raise RuntimeError("DataNotLoaded")

        self.assertIsNone(data.optional_data(Session(), "weather_data"))

    def test_telemetry_requires_both_position_and_car_channels(self):
        session = lap_session()
        session.car_data = {"1": pd.DataFrame({"Speed": [100]})}
        self.assertFalse(data._valid(session, True))
        session.pos_data = {"1": pd.DataFrame({"X": [100]})}
        self.assertTrue(data._valid(session, True))

    def test_system_exit_from_fastf1_cannot_kill_api_worker(self):
        session = SimpleNamespace(load=Mock(side_effect=SystemExit()))
        with self.assertRaises(SessionDataError) as caught:
            data._try_load(session, False)
        self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, "archive_cache_unusable")

    def test_upstream_error_is_not_converted_to_422(self):
        error = SessionDataError("archive_auth_required", "Run auth command")
        with patch.object(prediction, "load_session", side_effect=error):
            response = TestClient(app).post(
                "/api/prediction/run",
                json={
                    "year": 2026,
                    "gp": "Mexico City Grand Prix",
                    "session": "FP2",
                    "model": "v12",
                },
            )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 503)
        self.assertEqual(response.headers["x-f1-data-error"], "archive_auth_required")
        self.assertEqual(response.json()["detail"], "Run auth command")

    def test_failure_memo_does_not_keep_partial_session(self):
        with patch.object(
            f1_access,
            "auth_status",
            return_value={"authenticated": False, "credentialsConfigured": False},
        ), patch.object(
            data,
            "_session",
            side_effect=SessionDataError("archive_auth_required", "Login"),
        ) as loader:
            for _ in range(2):
                with self.assertRaises(SessionDataError):
                    data.load_session(2026, 19, "Q")
        loader.assert_called_once()


class ArchiveAccess(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_probe_distinguishes_auth_missing_and_rate_limit(self):
        cases = {
            401: ("archive_auth_rejected", 503),
            403: ("archive_access_denied", 503),
            404: ("session_not_published", 425),
            429: ("archive_rate_limited", 503),
            500: ("archive_upstream_error", 502),
        }
        for status, (code, http_status) in cases.items():
            with patch.object(
                fastf1.Cache,
                "requests_get",
                return_value=SimpleNamespace(status_code=status),
            ):
                with self.assertRaises(SessionDataError) as caught:
                    f1_access.probe_archive(template())
            self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, code)
            self.assertEqual(caught.exception.status_code, http_status)

    def test_no_browser_or_password_prompt_in_api_requests(self):
        from fastf1.internals import f1account
        from fastf1.exceptions import F1AuthError

        with patch.object(f1account.token_manager, "_default_auth_func"), patch.dict(
            "os.environ", {}, clear=True
        ):
            f1_access.install_server_policy()
            with self.assertRaises(F1AuthError):
                f1account.token_manager._default_auth_func()

    def test_cache_only_is_scoped_and_does_not_affect_other_threads(self):
        with patch.object(
            fastf1.Cache, "_request", return_value=SimpleNamespace(status_code=200)
        ) as request:
            with f1_access.cache_only():
                fastf1.Cache.requests_get("https://example.com")
                worker = threading.Thread(
                    target=lambda: fastf1.Cache.requests_get("https://example.com")
                )
                worker.start()
                worker.join()
            fastf1.Cache.requests_get("https://example.com")
        calls = request.call_args_list
        self.assertTrue(calls[0].kwargs["only_if_cached"])
        self.assertNotIn("only_if_cached", calls[1].kwargs)
        self.assertNotIn("only_if_cached", calls[2].kwargs)

    def test_existing_parsed_caches_are_merged_without_overwriting(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp, patch.dict(
            "os.environ", {}, clear=True
        ), patch.object(fastf1.Cache, "configure"):
            root = Path(temp)
            existing = root / "f1_cache" / "2026" / "event"
            existing.mkdir(parents=True)
            (existing / "timing.ff1pkl").write_bytes(b"good-cache")
            other = root / "formula1-ghost-car" / ".fastf1_cache" / "2026" / "event"
            other.mkdir(parents=True)
            (other / "timing.ff1pkl").write_bytes(b"do-not-overwrite")
            (other / "car.ff1pkl").write_bytes(b"car-cache")
            target = f1_access.configure_cache(root, root / "var")
            self.assertEqual(target, root / "f1_cache")
            self.assertEqual(
                (target / "2026/event/timing.ff1pkl").read_bytes(), b"good-cache"
            )
            self.assertEqual(
                (target / "2026/event/car.ff1pkl").read_bytes(), b"car-cache"
            )
            self.assertTrue((other / "car.ff1pkl").exists())

    def test_status_never_exposes_token_or_account(self):
        fake = SimpleNamespace(
            token_manager=SimpleNamespace(
                get_stored=lambda: SimpleNamespace(
                    value="SENSITIVE", is_valid=lambda: True
                )
            )
        )
        with patch.object(f1_access, "_auth_module", return_value=fake):
            state = f1_access.auth_status()
        self.assertTrue(state["authenticated"])
        self.assertNotIn("SENSITIVE", str(state))
        self.assertEqual(
            set(state), {"authenticated", "credentialsConfigured", "loginCommand"}
        )


class PublicResults(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        results._load.cache_clear()

    def load(self):
        stub = template()
        stub.load = Mock(side_effect=AssertionError("Raw API must not be used"))
        client = Mock()
        client.get_qualifying_results.return_value = public_response()
        with patch.object(fastf1, "get_session", return_value=stub), patch.object(
            results, "Ergast", return_value=client
        ):
            session = results.load_results_session(2026, 19, "Q")
        stub.load.assert_not_called()
        return session

    def test_published_results_need_no_raw_session_status_or_laps(self):
        session = self.load()
        self.assertTrue(data.finished(session))
        self.assertEqual(len(session.results), 2)
        self.assertFalse(hasattr(session, "laps"))
        gap = analysis.qualifying_gap(session, "AAA", "BBB")
        self.assertEqual(gap["segment"], "Q1")
        self.assertAlmostEqual(gap["deltaSeconds"], -0.2)

    def test_weekend_can_show_published_quali_while_race_timing_is_unavailable(self):
        session = self.load()
        error = SessionDataError("archive_auth_required", "Authenticate")
        with patch.object(
            analysis, "load_session", side_effect=[error, session, error]
        ):
            report = analysis.weekend(2026, 19)
        self.assertEqual(len(report["teams"]), 1)
        self.assertIsNone(report["teams"][0]["raceDeltaSeconds"])
        self.assertEqual(report["teams"][0]["matchedLaps"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(
            report["dataSources"]["Q"], "Jolpica-F1 published classification"
        )
        self.assertTrue(any("Authenticate" in w for w in report["warnings"]))

    def test_public_results_still_work_on_older_runtime(self):
        with patch.object(fastf1, "__version__", "3.7.0"):
            self.assertEqual(len(self.load().results), 2)

    def test_no_data_reports_not_published(self):
        client = Mock()
        client.get_qualifying_results.return_value = SimpleNamespace(content=[])
        with patch.object(fastf1, "get_session", return_value=template()), patch.object(
            results, "Ergast", return_value=client
        ):
            with self.assertRaises(SessionDataError) as caught:
                results.load_results_session(2026, 19, "Q")
        self.assertEqual(caught.exception.status_code, 425)

    def test_upstream_failures_do_not_become_weekend_404(self):
        with patch(
            "backend.routers.weekend.weekend",
            side_effect=SessionDataError(
                "archive_network_error", "Network unavailable", 502
            ),
        ):
            response = TestClient(app).get("/api/weekend/2026/19")
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 502)
        self.assertEqual(response.json()["detail"], "Network unavailable")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


============================================================
