FILE: ./frontend/src/pages/MotorsportPage.tsx
============================================================
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import ICAL from "ical.js";
import type { CalendarEvent, CalendarResponse } from "../api";
import { useApi } from "../hooks/useApi";
import DataState from "../components/DataState";
import CalendarList, { browserZone } from "../components/CalendarList";

const SERIES: Record<string, string> = {
  f1: "Formula 1",
  wec: "WEC · Hypercar / LMGT3",
  imsa: "IMSA WeatherTech",
  nls: "Nürburgring NLS",
  "gt-europe": "GT World Challenge Europe",
  supercars: "Supercars",
};
const STORAGE = "f1-addiction-calendar-imports-v1";
function saved(): CalendarEvent[] {
  try {
    const value = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE) ?? "[]");
    return Array.isArray(value)
      ? value.filter(
          (e) =>
            typeof e.start === "string" &&
            typeof e.series === "string" &&
            typeof e.sourceUrl === "string" &&
            e.sourceUrl.startsWith("https://"),
        )
      : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}
export default function MotorsportPage() {
  const [year, setYear] = useState(new Date().getFullYear());
  const [month, setMonth] = useState("");
  const [active, setActive] = useState(Object.keys(SERIES));
  const [zone, setZone] = useState(browserZone());
  const [imports, setImports] = useState<CalendarEvent[]>(saved);
  const [series, setSeries] = useState("wec");
  const [source, setSource] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");
  const report = useApi<CalendarResponse>(`/calendar/motorsport/${year}`);
  const events = useMemo(() => {
    const overrides = new Set(
      imports.filter((e) => +e.start.slice(0, 4) === year).map((e) => e.series),
    );
    return [
      ...(report.data?.events ?? []).filter((e) => !overrides.has(e.series)),
      ...imports.filter((e) => +e.start.slice(0, 4) === year),
    ]
      .filter((e) => active.includes(e.series))
      .filter((e) => {
        if (!month) return true;
        if (e.dateOnly) {
          const end = new Date(`${(e.end ?? e.start).slice(0, 10)}T12:00:00Z`);
          if (e.end) end.setUTCDate(end.getUTCDate() - 1);
          const selectedMonth = `${year}-${month}`;
          return (
            e.start.slice(0, 7) <= selectedMonth &&
            end.toISOString().slice(0, 7) >= selectedMonth
          );
        }
        return (
          new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
            timeZone: zone,
            month: "2-digit",
          }).format(new Date(e.start)) === month
        );
      })
      .sort((a, b) => a.start.localeCompare(b.start));
  }, [report.data, imports, year, month, active, zone]);
  async function importFile(file: File | undefined) {
    if (!file) return;
    setError("");
    setStatus("");
    try {
      const url = new URL(source);
      if (url.protocol !== "https:")
        throw new Error(
          "Use an HTTPS publisher link to identify the calendar source.",
        );
      if (file.size > 2_000_000)
        throw new Error("Choose a calendar smaller than 2 MB.");
      const root = new ICAL.Component(ICAL.parse(await file.text()));
      root
        .getAllSubcomponents("vtimezone")
        .forEach((c) => ICAL.TimezoneService.register(new ICAL.Timezone(c)));
      const components = root.getAllSubcomponents("vevent");
      if (components.length > 2000)
        throw new Error("Import at most 2,000 sessions at once.");
      const parsed: CalendarEvent[] = components.flatMap((c) => {
        if (c.getFirstPropertyValue("status") === "CANCELLED") return [];
        if (c.hasProperty("rrule") || c.hasProperty("recurrence-id"))
          throw new Error(
            "Import a calendar with individual sessions; recurring entries need expansion first.",
          );
        const e = new ICAL.Event(c),
          start = e.startDate,
          end = e.endDate;
        if (!start) throw new Error("An event has no start date.");
        if (!start.isDate && start.zone.tzid === "floating")
          throw new Error(
            "A session has no timezone. Download a calendar with UTC or TZID times.",
          );
        if (
          !start.isDate &&
          start.zone.tzid !== "UTC" &&
          start.zone.tzid !== "Z" &&
          !root
            .getAllSubcomponents("vtimezone")
            .some((t) => t.getFirstPropertyValue("tzid") === start.zone.tzid)
        )
          throw new Error(
            "Calendar timezone definition is missing. Use a UTC export or one containing VTIMEZONE.",
          );
        const stamp = start.isDate
          ? start.toString()
          : start.toJSDate().toISOString();
        if (+stamp.slice(0, 4) !== year) return [];
        return [
          {
            id: `import-${series}-${e.uid || stamp}`,
            series,
            title: e.summary || "Session",
            event: e.summary || "Session",
            start: stamp,
            end: end
              ? end.isDate
                ? end.toString()
                : end.toJSDate().toISOString()
              : null,
            dateOnly: start.isDate,
            location: e.location || "",
            source: "Your imported calendar",
            sourceUrl: url.href,
            verifiedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
            status: "Imported publisher schedule",
          },
        ];
      });
      if (!parsed.length)
        throw new Error(`No sessions for ${year} in this file.`);
      const next = [
        ...imports.filter(
          (e) => e.series !== series || +e.start.slice(0, 4) !== year,
        ),
        ...parsed,
      ];
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE, JSON.stringify(next));
      setImports(next);
      setStatus(
        `Imported ${parsed.length} entries for ${SERIES[series]}. This browser keeps your copy.`,
      );
    } catch (e: unknown) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Calendar import failed");
    }
  }
  return (
    <>
      <div className="page__header">
        <p className="eyebrow">NOT JUST F1</p>
        <h1 className="page__title">Your year in racing</h1>
        <p className="page__subtitle">
          Endurance, GT and touring cars in one place. Hypercar is included
          within WEC; IMSA prototypes appear within IMSA.
        </p>
      </div>
      <div className="control-panel control-panel__row">
        <div className="form-group">
          <label htmlFor="calendar-year">Year</label>
          <input
            id="calendar-year"
            className="form-select"
            type="number"
            min="2018"
            max="2100"
            value={year}
            onChange={(e) => setYear(+e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="form-group">
          <label htmlFor="month">Month</label>
          <select
            id="month"
            className="form-select"
            value={month}
            onChange={(e) => setMonth(e.target.value)}
          >
            <option value="">Whole year</option>
            {Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => (
              <option key={i} value={String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0")}>
                {new Date(2026, i, 1).toLocaleString(undefined, {
                  month: "long",
                })}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="form-group">
          <label htmlFor="timezone">Display timezone</label>
          <select
            id="timezone"
            className="form-select"
            value={zone}
            onChange={(e) => setZone(e.target.value)}
          >
            {[
              ...new Set([
                browserZone(),
                "UTC",
                "Australia/Brisbane",
                "Australia/Sydney",
                "Europe/London",
                "Europe/Berlin",
                "America/New_York",
                "America/Los_Angeles",
                "Asia/Tokyo",
              ]),
            ].map((z) => (
              <option key={z}>{z}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="series-filters">
        {Object.entries(SERIES).map(([key, label]) => (
          <label key={key}>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={active.includes(key)}
              onChange={() =>
                setActive((v) =>
                  v.includes(key) ? v.filter((k) => k !== key) : [...v, key],
                )
              }
            />
            {label}
          </label>
        ))}
      </div>
      <p className="notice">
        Timed sessions use {zone}, including daylight-saving rules. Date-only
        events retain venue dates and show “session time TBC”. Other-series
        dates are a checked-in 2026 snapshot; use the linked organiser schedule
        for changes.
      </p>
      <DataState {...report} />
      {report.data?.warnings?.map((w) => (
        <p className="notice" key={w}>
          {w}
        </p>
      ))}
      <CalendarList events={events} zone={zone} />
      <details className="methodology">
        <summary>Add or update a series from a publisher calendar</summary>
        <p>
          Download an .ics calendar from the organiser and import it here. It
          replaces this series’ displayed entries for the selected year in this
          browser.
        </p>
        <div className="control-panel__row">
          <select
            aria-label="Import series"
            className="form-select"
            value={series}
            onChange={(e) => setSeries(e.target.value)}
          >
            {Object.entries(SERIES)
              .filter(([k]) => k !== "f1")
              .map(([k, v]) => (
                <option key={k} value={k}>
                  {v}
                </option>
              ))}
          </select>
          <input
            className="form-select"
            type="url"
            aria-label="Publisher source URL"
            placeholder="https://organiser.example/calendar"
            value={source}
            onChange={(e) => setSource(e.target.value)}
          />
          <input
            aria-label="Calendar file"
            type="file"
            accept=".ics,text/calendar"
            onChange={(e) => {
              void importFile(e.target.files?.[0]);
              e.target.value = "";
            }}
          />
        </div>
        <p role="alert" className="text-red">
          {error}
        </p>
        <p role="status">{status}</p>
        {imports.length > 0 && (
          <button
            className="btn btn--secondary"
            onClick={() => {
              localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE);
              setImports([]);
              setStatus(
                "Imported calendars cleared; publisher snapshots restored.",
              );
            }}
          >
            Clear imported calendars
          </button>
        )}
      </details>
    </>
  );
}

FILE: ./frontend/src/pages/BestCarPage.tsx
============================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  ResponsiveContainer,
  LineChart,
  Line,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  Tooltip,
  ReferenceLine,
  CartesianGrid,
} from "recharts";
import type {
  EraConfig,
  EngineeringTopic,
  EventInfo,
  DriverInfo,
  ComparisonResponse,
} from "../api";
import { useApi } from "../hooks/useApi";
import { useSeasonAnalysis } from "../hooks/useSeasonAnalysis";
import DataState from "../components/DataState";
import EngineeringCar from "../components/EngineeringCar";
import { ComparisonView } from "./GhostCarPage";

interface Progression {
  progression: {
    round: number;
    race: string;
    gap: number;
    gapPercent: number;
  }[];
  unavailable: string[];
  method: string;
}
export default function BestCarPage() {
  const [eraKey, setEraKey] = useState("2025"),
    [topic, setTopic] = useState(0),
    [round, setRound] = useState("");
  const [first, setFirst] = useState(""),
    [second, setSecond] = useState(""),
    [comparisonPath, setComparisonPath] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const eras = useApi<{ eras: Record<string, string> }>("/best-car/eras");
  const era = useApi<EraConfig>(`/best-car/era/${eraKey}`);
  const topics = useApi<{
    eras: Record<string, EngineeringTopic[]>;
    note: string;
  }>("/engineering/topics");
  const progression = useSeasonAnalysis<Progression>(eraKey);
  const schedule = useApi<{ events: EventInfo[] }>(
    era.data ? `/schedule/${era.data.year}` : null,
  );
  const drivers = useApi<{ drivers: DriverInfo[] }>(
    era.data && round ? `/drivers/${era.data.year}/${round}/Q` : null,
  );
  const comparison = useApi<ComparisonResponse>(comparisonPath);
  const active = topics.data?.eras[eraKey]?.[topic];
  useEffect(() => {
    setRound(
      String(
        schedule.data?.events.find((e) => !e.isTesting)?.roundNumber ?? "",
      ),
    );
  }, [schedule.data]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const list = drivers.data?.drivers ?? [];
    setFirst(
      list.find((d) => d.team === era.data?.primaryCar.team)?.abbreviation ??
        list[0]?.abbreviation ??
        "",
    );
    setSecond(
      list.find((d) => d.team !== era.data?.primaryCar.team)?.abbreviation ??
        list[1]?.abbreviation ??
        "",
    );
  }, [drivers.data, era.data]);
  useEffect(() => setComparisonPath(null), [eraKey, round, first, second]);
  return (
    <>
      <div className="page__header">
        <p className="eyebrow">LOOKING BACK</p>
        <h1 className="page__title">The engineering behind the lap</h1>
        <p className="page__subtitle">
          Explore documented design decisions alongside observed performance.
          Timing evidence shows outcomes; it cannot isolate the effect of a
          single component.
        </p>
      </div>
      <div className="control-panel">
        <label htmlFor="era">Car / era</label>
        <select
          id="era"
          className="form-select"
          value={eraKey}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setEraKey(e.target.value);
            setTopic(0);
            setRound("");
          }}
        >
          {Object.entries(eras.data?.eras ?? {}).map(([k, v]) => (
            <option key={k} value={k}>
              {v}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>
      <DataState {...era} />
      <DataState {...topics} />
      {era.data && (
        <div className="grid-3">
          {[era.data.primaryCar, ...era.data.competitors].map((car) => (
            <article
              className="model-card"
              key={car.car}
              style={{ borderTop: `3px solid ${car.color}` }}
            >
              <h2>{car.car}</h2>
              <p>
                {car.team} · {car.drivers.join(" / ")}
              </p>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      {active && (
        <section className="engineering-layout">
          <EngineeringCar focus={active.id} />
          <div>
            <div className="button-group">
              {topics.data?.eras[eraKey].map((t, i) => (
                <button
                  key={t.id}
                  className={`btn ${topic === i ? "btn--primary" : "btn--secondary"}`}
                  onClick={() => setTopic(i)}
                >
                  {t.id}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <h2>{active.title}</h2>
            <p>{active.documented}</p>
            <h3>What to inspect in the data</h3>
            <p>{active.measure}</p>
            <p className="notice">{active.cannotInfer}</p>
            <a
              className="source-link"
              href={active.sourceUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noreferrer"
            >
              {active.sourceLabel} ↗
            </a>
          </div>
        </section>
      )}
      <section className="visual-panel">
        <h2>Season advantage against the closest rival</h2>
        <p className="text-gray">
          Best primary-team Q1 lap minus best other-team Q1 lap. Negative means
          the primary team was faster. Percentage gaps account for lap length,
          while circuit and weather effects remain.
        </p>
        <DataState {...progression} />
        {progression.loading && (
          <p className="text-gray">
            {progression.progress} · first load downloads the season in the
            background.
          </p>
        )}
        {progression.data && (
          <>
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={300}>
              <LineChart data={progression.data.progression}>
                <CartesianGrid stroke="#39394b" />
                <XAxis
                  dataKey="round"
                  label={{
                    value: "Round",
                    position: "insideBottom",
                    offset: -2,
                  }}
                />
                <YAxis unit="%" />
                <Tooltip
                  contentStyle={{ background: "#242433" }}
                  labelFormatter={(v) => `Round ${v}`}
                />
                <ReferenceLine y={0} stroke="#ddd" />
                <Line
                  name="Gap to rival (%)"
                  dataKey="gapPercent"
                  stroke={era.data?.primaryCar.color ?? "#f66"}
                  dot
                />
              </LineChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
            {progression.data.unavailable.length > 0 && (
              <p className="notice">
                Missing rounds: {progression.data.unavailable.join(", ")}. No
                values were substituted.
              </p>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </section>
      <section className="visual-panel">
        <h2>Inspect the recorded laps</h2>
        <p className="text-gray">
          Compare any two drivers in the same qualifying session. Their fastest
          laps may come from different Q segments; use Weekend by Weekend for a
          shared-segment teammate comparison.
        </p>
        <div className="control-panel__row">
          <select
            aria-label="Historical race"
            className="form-select"
            value={round}
            onChange={(e) => setRound(e.target.value)}
          >
            {schedule.data?.events
              .filter((e) => !e.isTesting)
              .map((e) => (
                <option key={e.roundNumber} value={e.roundNumber}>
                  {e.eventName}
                </option>
              ))}
          </select>
          {[
            { value: first, set: setFirst, label: "Historical driver A" },
            { value: second, set: setSecond, label: "Historical driver B" },
          ].map((c) => (
            <select
              key={c.label}
              aria-label={c.label}
              className="form-select"
              value={c.value}
              onChange={(e) => c.set(e.target.value)}
            >
              {drivers.data?.drivers.map((d) => (
                <option key={d.abbreviation} value={d.abbreviation}>
                  {d.abbreviation} · {d.team}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          ))}
          <button
            className="btn btn--primary"
            disabled={
              !era.data || !round || !first || !second || first === second
            }
            onClick={() =>
              setComparisonPath(
                `/ghost-car/compare/${era.data!.year}/${round}/Q/${first}/${second}`,
              )
            }
          >
            Load telemetry
          </button>
        </div>
        <DataState {...schedule} />
        <DataState {...drivers} />
        <DataState {...comparison} />
        {comparison.data && <ComparisonView data={comparison.data} />}
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import type { StandingsResponse, CalendarResponse } from "../api";
import { useApi } from "../hooks/useApi";
import SeasonSelect from "../components/SeasonSelect";
import DataState from "../components/DataState";
import StandingsCircuit from "../components/StandingsCircuit";
import CalendarList, {
  browserZone,
  formatEvent,
} from "../components/CalendarList";

export default function HomePage() {
  const [year, setYear] = useState(new Date().getFullYear());
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<"drivers" | "constructors">("drivers");
  const [all, setAll] = useState(false);
  const [tick, setTick] = useState(Date.now());
  const [refresh, setRefresh] = useState(0);
  const standings = useApi<StandingsResponse>(`/standings/${year}`, refresh);
  const calendar = useApi<CalendarResponse>(`/calendar/f1/${year}`, refresh);
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => setTick(Date.now()), 1000);
    const reload = setInterval(() => setRefresh((v) => v + 1), 300000);
    return () => {
      clearInterval(timer);
      clearInterval(reload);
    };
  }, []);
  const next = calendar.data?.events.find(
    (e) => new Date(e.start).getTime() > tick,
  );
  const remaining = next
    ? Math.max(0, Math.floor((new Date(next.start).getTime() - tick) / 1000))
    : 0;
  const rows = standings.data?.[kind] ?? [];
  const zone = browserZone();
  return (
    <>
      <div className="page__header">
        <p className="eyebrow">THE SEASON, IN CONTEXT</p>
        <h1 className="page__title">The championship picture</h1>
        <p className="page__subtitle">
          Published standings, movement since the previous Grand Prix, and your
          next lights out.
        </p>
      </div>
      <div className="control-panel control-panel__row">
        <SeasonSelect value={year} onChange={setYear} />
        <button
          className="btn btn--secondary"
          onClick={() => setRefresh((v) => v + 1)}
        >
          Refresh data
        </button>
      </div>
      <section className="next-session">
        <p className="eyebrow">NEXT F1 SESSION · {zone}</p>
        {next ? (
          <>
            <h2>{next.title}</h2>
            <p>{formatEvent(next, zone)}</p>
            <strong className="countdown">
              {Math.floor(remaining / 86400)}d{" "}
              {Math.floor(remaining / 3600) % 24}h{" "}
              {Math.floor(remaining / 60) % 60}m {remaining % 60}s
            </strong>
          </>
        ) : (
          <p>
            {calendar.loading
              ? "Checking the schedule…"
              : `No later published session in ${year}. Select next season when its schedule is released.`}
          </p>
        )}
      </section>
      <DataState {...standings} />
      {standings.data && (
        <>
          <div className="section-heading">
            <div>
              <h2>Standings after round {standings.data.round ?? "—"}</h2>
              <p className="text-gray">
                {standings.data.comparison} Retrieved{" "}
                {new Date(standings.data.asOf).toLocaleString()}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="button-group">
              {(["drivers", "constructors"] as const).map((k) => (
                <button
                  className={`btn ${kind === k ? "btn--primary" : "btn--secondary"}`}
                  key={k}
                  aria-pressed={kind === k}
                  onClick={() => setKind(k)}
                >
                  {k === "drivers" ? "Drivers" : "Constructors"}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          {rows.length > 0 && (
            <StandingsCircuit
              key={kind}
              rows={rows}
              round={standings.data.round}
              previousRound={standings.data.previousRound}
            />
          )}
          <div className="table-scroll">
            <table className="f1-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Pos</th>
                  <th>{kind === "drivers" ? "Driver" : "Constructor"}</th>
                  <th>Team</th>
                  <th>Points</th>
                  <th>Δ points</th>
                  <th>Δ places</th>
                  <th>Wins</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {rows.map((r) => (
                  <tr key={r.id}>
                    <td>{r.position}</td>
                    <td className="font-bold">{r.name}</td>
                    <td>{r.team}</td>
                    <td>{r.points}</td>
                    <td>
                      {r.pointsChange == null
                        ? "—"
                        : `${r.pointsChange > 0 ? "+" : ""}${r.pointsChange}`}
                    </td>
                    <td
                      className={(r.positionChange ?? 0) > 0 ? "positive" : ""}
                    >
                      {r.positionChange == null
                        ? "—"
                        : `${r.positionChange > 0 ? "↑ " : r.positionChange < 0 ? "↓ " : ""}${Math.abs(r.positionChange)}`}
                    </td>
                    <td>{r.wins}</td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
          {!rows.length && (
            <p className="notice">
              No standings published for this season yet.
            </p>
          )}
        </>
      )}
      <div className="section-heading">
        <h2>F1 calendar · your local time</h2>
        <button
          className="btn btn--secondary"
          onClick={() => setAll((v) => !v)}
        >
          {all ? "Show upcoming" : "Show full year"}
        </button>
      </div>
      <DataState {...calendar} />
      <CalendarList
        events={(calendar.data?.events ?? [])
          .filter((e) => all || new Date(e.start).getTime() > tick)
          .slice(0, all ? 200 : 10)}
        zone={zone}
      />
      <Link className="racing-link" to="/not-just-f1">
        <p className="eyebrow">NOT JUST F1</p>
        <h2>One calendar. A whole world of racing. ↗</h2>
        <p>WEC / Hypercar · IMSA · NLS · GT Europe · Supercars</p>
      </Link>
    </>
  );
}

FILE: ./frontend/src/components/TrackMap.tsx
============================================================
import { useMemo } from 'react'

interface Props {
  x1: number[]
  y1: number[]
  x2?: number[]
  y2?: number[]
  label1: string
  label2?: string
}

/**
 * Render an SVG track map with one or two driver paths.
 * Coordinates are normalised to fit a 1000×600 viewBox.
 */
export default function TrackMap({ x1, y1, x2, y2, label1, label2 }: Props) {
  const { path1, path2 } = useMemo(() => {
    // Combine all points for shared bounds
    const allX = x2 ? [...x1, ...x2] : x1
    const allY = y2 ? [...y1, ...y2] : y1
    const minX = Math.min(...allX)
    const maxX = Math.max(...allX)
    const minY = Math.min(...allY)
    const maxY = Math.max(...allY)
    const rangeX = maxX - minX || 1
    const rangeY = maxY - minY || 1

    const PAD = 40
    const W = 1000 - PAD * 2
    const H = 600 - PAD * 2

    const scale = Math.min(W / rangeX, H / rangeY)
    const offsetX = PAD + (W - rangeX * scale) / 2
    const offsetY = PAD + (H - rangeY * scale) / 2

    const norm = (xs: number[], ys: number[]) => {
      // Sample every 3rd point for performance
      const step = Math.max(1, Math.floor(xs.length / 400))
      const pts: string[] = []
      for (let i = 0; i < xs.length; i += step) {
        const nx = offsetX + (xs[i] - minX) * scale
        const ny = offsetY + (ys[i] - minY) * scale
        pts.push(`${nx.toFixed(1)},${ny.toFixed(1)}`)
      }
      return pts.join(' ')
    }

    return {
      path1: norm(x1, y1),
      path2: x2 && y2 ? norm(x2, y2) : undefined,
    }
  }, [x1, y1, x2, y2])

  return (
    <div className="track-map">
      <svg viewBox="0 0 1000 600" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet">
        {/* Track outline (driver 1 path acts as reference) */}
        <polyline
          points={path1}
          fill="none"
          stroke="#333"
          strokeWidth="10"
          strokeLinecap="round"
          strokeLinejoin="round"
        />

        {/* Driver 2 path */}
        {path2 && (
          <polyline
            points={path2}
            fill="none"
            stroke="var(--team-mercedes)"
            strokeWidth="3"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
            opacity={0.8}
          />
        )}

        {/* Driver 1 path */}
        <polyline
          points={path1}
          fill="none"
          stroke="var(--f1-red)"
          strokeWidth="3"
          strokeLinecap="round"
          strokeLinejoin="round"
          opacity={0.9}
        />

        {/* Legend */}
        <rect x="20" y="560" width="12" height="12" rx="2" fill="var(--f1-red)" />
        <text x="38" y="571" fill="var(--f1-white)" fontSize="12" fontFamily="Titillium Web, sans-serif">{label1}</text>

        {label2 && (
          <>
            <rect x="100" y="560" width="12" height="12" rx="2" fill="var(--team-mercedes)" />
            <text x="118" y="571" fill="var(--f1-white)" fontSize="12" fontFamily="Titillium Web, sans-serif">{label2}</text>
          </>
        )}
      </svg>
    </div>
  )
}

import { useState } from "react";
export default function EngineeringCar({ focus }: { focus: string }) {
  const [moving, setMoving] = useState(true);
  return (
    <div className={`engineering-car ${moving ? "" : "paused"}`}>
      <svg
        viewBox="0 0 700 480"
        role="img"
        aria-label={`Conceptual top-down car schematic highlighting ${focus}`}
      >
        <defs>
          <marker
            id="air-arrow"
            viewBox="0 0 10 10"
            refX="8"
            refY="5"
            markerWidth="5"
            markerHeight="5"
            orient="auto"
          >
            <path d="M0 0 L10 5 L0 10" fill="#6be2ff" />
          </marker>
        </defs>
        <path
          d="M280 105 L420 105 L440 355 L400 405 L300 405 L260 355 Z"
          fill={focus === "floor" ? "#25608b" : "#303044"}
          stroke="#72728a"
          strokeWidth="3"
        />
        <path
          d="M340 50 L360 50 L380 280 L370 385 L330 385 L320 280 Z"
          fill="#ed3535"
        />
        <ellipse cx="350" cy="230" rx="20" ry="35" fill="#11111c" />
        {[240, 420].flatMap((x) =>
          [130, 330].map((y) => (
            <rect
              key={`${x}-${y}`}
              x={x}
              y={y}
              width="40"
              height="65"
              rx="8"
              fill="#101018"
              stroke="#777"
            />
          )),
        )}
        <rect x="240" y="65" width="220" height="16" rx="3" fill="#b0b0c3" />
        <rect x="265" y="405" width="170" height="18" rx="3" fill="#b0b0c3" />
        {focus === "brakes" &&
          [275, 425].flatMap((x) =>
            [160, 360].map((y) => (
              <circle key={`${x}-${y}`} cx={x} cy={y} r="13" fill="#ff8700" />
            )),
          )}
        {focus === "suspension" &&
          [150, 345].map((y) => (
            <path
              key={y}
              d={`M275 ${y} L330 ${y + 25} L275 ${y + 40} M425 ${y} L370 ${y + 25} L425 ${y + 40}`}
              stroke="#27f4d2"
              strokeWidth="6"
              fill="none"
            />
          ))}
        {focus === "engine" && (
          <rect x="320" y="285" width="60" height="75" rx="8" fill="#f3be41" />
        )}
        {[215, 295, 405, 485].map((x, i) => (
          <path
            className="airflow"
            key={x}
            d={`M${x} 10 Q${x + (i < 2 ? -30 : 30)} 190 ${x} 455`}
            fill="none"
            stroke="#6be2ff"
            strokeWidth="3"
            strokeDasharray="12 14"
            markerEnd="url(#air-arrow)"
          />
        ))}
        <text x="350" y="465" fill="#aaaabe" textAnchor="middle" fontSize="14">
          CONCEPT SCHEMATIC · NOT CFD OR MEASURED AIRFLOW
        </text>
      </svg>
      <button
        className="btn btn--secondary"
        onClick={() => setMoving((v) => !v)}
      >
        {moving ? "Pause concept animation" : "Animate concept"}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

============================================================
FILE: ./frontend/src/components/LapReplay.tsx
============================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { LapData, TelemetryData } from "../api";
import CarGlyph, { teamColor } from "./CarGlyph";

function sample(tel: TelemetryData, elapsed: number) {
  const times = tel.time,
    offset = times[0] ?? 0;
  let i = 0;
  while (i < times.length - 2 && times[i + 1] - offset < elapsed) i++;
  const j = Math.min(i + 1, times.length - 1),
    a = times[i] - offset,
    b = times[j] - offset;
  const fraction = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (elapsed - a) / (b - a || 1)));
  return {
    x: tel.x[i] + (tel.x[j] - tel.x[i]) * fraction,
    y: tel.y[i] + (tel.y[j] - tel.y[i]) * fraction,
    speed: tel.speed[i],
    gear: tel.gear[i],
  };
}
export default function LapReplay({
  first,
  second,
}: {
  first: LapData;
  second: LapData;
}) {
  const [time, setTime] = useState(0),
    [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false),
    [speed, setSpeed] = useState(1);
  const clock = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const duration = Math.max(
    first.telemetry.time.at(-1)! - first.telemetry.time[0],
    second.telemetry.time.at(-1)! - second.telemetry.time[0],
  );
  useEffect(() => {
    setTime(0);
    setPlaying(false);
  }, [first, second]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!playing) return;
    let handle = 0;
    clock.current = null;
    const animate = (now: number) => {
      if (clock.current != null) {
        const dt = ((now - clock.current) / 1000) * speed;
        setTime((t) => Math.min(duration, t + dt));
      }
      clock.current = now;
      handle = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    };
    handle = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(handle);
  }, [playing, speed, duration]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (time >= duration) setPlaying(false);
  }, [time, duration]);
  const bounds = useMemo(() => {
    const xs = [...first.telemetry.x, ...second.telemetry.x],
      ys = [...first.telemetry.y, ...second.telemetry.y];
    const minX = Math.min(...xs),
      maxX = Math.max(...xs),
      minY = Math.min(...ys),
      maxY = Math.max(...ys);
    const scale = Math.min(820 / (maxX - minX || 1), 430 / (maxY - minY || 1));
    return { minX, minY, scale };
  }, [first, second]);
  const norm = (x: number, y: number) => ({
    x: 80 + (x - bounds.minX) * bounds.scale,
    y: 490 - (y - bounds.minY) * bounds.scale,
  });
  const path = first.telemetry.x
    .map((x, i) => {
      const p = norm(x, first.telemetry.y[i]);
      return `${p.x},${p.y}`;
    })
    .join(" ");
  return (
    <section className="visual-panel">
      <div className="section-heading">
        <h2>Bird’s-eye lap replay</h2>
        <span className="tag">Recorded position samples</span>
      </div>
      <p className="text-gray">
        Both fastest laps start together at t=0. Position is interpolated
        between samples; this is not a reconstruction of an on-track battle.
      </p>
      <svg
        viewBox="0 0 1000 550"
        className="lap-replay"
        role="img"
        aria-label="Two fastest laps replayed on the recorded circuit"
      >
        <polyline
          points={path}
          fill="none"
          stroke="#48485d"
          strokeWidth="18"
          strokeLinejoin="round"
        />
        {[first, second].map((d, index) => {
          const point = sample(
            d.telemetry,
            Math.min(time, d.telemetry.time.at(-1)! - d.telemetry.time[0]),
          );
          const p = norm(point.x, point.y),
            next = sample(
              d.telemetry,
              Math.min(
                time + 0.2,
                d.telemetry.time.at(-1)! - d.telemetry.time[0],
              ),
            ),
            nextP = norm(next.x, next.y);
          const angle =
            (Math.atan2(nextP.y - p.y, nextP.x - p.x) * 180) / Math.PI + 90;
          return (
            <g key={index} transform={`translate(${p.x} ${p.y})`}>
              <g transform={`rotate(${angle}) scale(.8)`}>
                <CarGlyph color={teamColor(d.team)} />
              </g>
              <text
                x="20"
                y={index ? 22 : -15}
                fill={teamColor(d.team)}
                fontSize="17"
                fontWeight="bold"
              >
                {d.abbreviation ?? d.driver}
              </text>
            </g>
          );
        })}
      </svg>
      <div className="replay-controls">
        <button
          className="btn btn--primary"
          onClick={() => {
            if (time >= duration) setTime(0);
            setPlaying((v) => !v);
          }}
        >
          {playing ? "Pause" : "Play"}
        </button>
        <button
          className="btn btn--secondary"
          onClick={() => {
            setTime(0);
            setPlaying(false);
          }}
        >
          Reset
        </button>
        <input
          aria-label="Lap playback position"
          type="range"
          min="0"
          max={duration}
          step="0.05"
          value={time}
          onChange={(e) => setTime(+e.target.value)}
        />
        <span>
          {time.toFixed(1)} / {duration.toFixed(1)} s
        </span>
        <select
          aria-label="Playback speed"
          className="form-select"
          value={speed}
          onChange={(e) => setSpeed(+e.target.value)}
        >
          {[0.25, 0.5, 1, 2, 4].map((v) => (
            <option key={v} value={v}>
              {v}×
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

FILE: ./frontend/src/components/CalendarList.tsx
============================================================
import type { CalendarEvent } from "../api";
export const browserZone = () =>
  Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone || "UTC";
export function formatEvent(event: CalendarEvent, zone: string) {
  if (event.dateOnly) {
    const fmt = (d: Date) =>
      d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
        timeZone: "UTC",
        month: "short",
        day: "numeric",
      });
    const start = new Date(`${event.start.slice(0, 10)}T12:00:00Z`);
    const end = event.end
      ? new Date(`${event.end.slice(0, 10)}T12:00:00Z`)
      : start;
    if (event.end) end.setUTCDate(end.getUTCDate() - 1);
    return `${fmt(start)}${end > start ? ` – ${fmt(end)}` : ""} · venue dates; session time TBC`;
  }
  return new Date(event.start).toLocaleString(undefined, {
    timeZone: zone,
    weekday: "short",
    month: "short",
    day: "numeric",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
    timeZoneName: "short",
  });
}
export default function CalendarList({
  events,
  zone,
}: {
  events: CalendarEvent[];
  zone: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="calendar-list">
      {events.map((event) => (
        <article className="calendar-row" key={event.id}>
          <span className={`series-pill series-${event.series}`}>
            {event.series.toUpperCase()}
          </span>
          <div>
            <strong>{event.title}</strong>
            <p>{formatEvent(event, zone)}</p>
            <small className="text-gray">
              {event.source} · checked {event.verifiedAt.slice(0, 10)}
            </small>
          </div>
          <a href={event.sourceUrl} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
            Source ↗
          </a>
        </article>
      ))}
      {!events.length && (
        <p className="notice">No published sessions in this selection.</p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: ./frontend/src/components/SeasonSelect.tsx
============================================================
import { useApi } from "../hooks/useApi";
export default function SeasonSelect({
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  value: number;
  onChange: (year: number) => void;
}) {
  const { data, error } = useApi<{ seasons: number[] }>("/seasons");
  const years = [...new Set([...(data?.seasons ?? []), value])].sort(
    (a, b) => a - b,
  );
  return (
    <div className="form-group">
      <label htmlFor="season">Season</label>
      <select
        id="season"
        className="form-select"
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(+e.target.value)}
      >
        {[...years].reverse().map((y) => (
          <option key={y}>{y}</option>
        ))}
      </select>
      {error && <small role="alert">{error}</small>}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: ./frontend/src/components/CarGlyph.tsx

/** Original schematic icon; no team logos or proprietary geometry. Nose points up. */
export default function CarGlyph({
  color,
  label,
}: {
  color: string;
  label?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <g>
      <rect x="-12" y="-17" width="24" height="5" rx="1" fill={color} />
      <rect x="-10" y="13" width="20" height="5" rx="1" fill={color} />
      <path
        d="M-3 -15 L3 -15 L7 10 L4 15 L-4 15 L-7 10 Z"
        fill={color}
        stroke="#fff"
        strokeWidth=".6"
      />
      <ellipse cx="0" cy="2" rx="3" ry="5" fill="#171721" />
      {[-1, 1].flatMap((side) =>
        [-10, 10].map((y) => (
          <rect
            key={`${side}-${y}`}
            x={side * 11 - 3}
            y={y - 4}
            width="6"
            height="9"
            rx="1"
            fill="#101015"
            stroke="#868698"
            strokeWidth=".5"
          />
        )),
      )}
      {label && (
        <text y="32" textAnchor="middle" fontSize="12" fill="#fff">
          {label}
        </text>
      )}
    </g>
  );
}
export function teamColor(team: string) {
  const lower = team.toLowerCase();
  return lower.includes("mclaren")
    ? "#ff8700"
    : lower.includes("ferrari")
      ? "#ef3340"
      : lower.includes("mercedes")
        ? "#27f4d2"
        : lower.includes("red bull")
          ? "#438cff"
          : lower.includes("williams")
            ? "#64c4ff"
            : lower.includes("aston")
              ? "#37b69c"
              : lower.includes("alpine")
                ? "#fa86cb"
                : "#c4c9d6";
}

FILE: ./frontend/src/components/DataState.tsx
============================================================
import Spinner from "./Spinner";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { ApiError } from "../api";

export default function DataState({
  loading,
  error,
  apiError,
  retry,
}: {
  loading: boolean;
  error: string;
  apiError?: ApiError | null;
  retry?: () => void;
}) {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(Date.now);
  const isPending = apiError?.status === 425;
  const retryAt = isPending ? apiError.retryAt : null;
  useEffect(() => {
    let timer: number | undefined;
    function updateAvailability() {
      const current = Date.now();
      setNow(current);
      if (retryAt !== null && retryAt > current) {
        timer = window.setTimeout(updateAvailability,
          Math.min(retryAt - current + 50, 2_147_483_647));
      }
    }
    updateAvailability();
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [retryAt]);
  const title = apiError?.code === "session_not_started"
    ? "This session hasn't started yet"
    : apiError?.code === "classification_not_published"
      ? "Session results aren't published yet"
      : "Session data isn't available yet";

  return (
    <>
      {loading && <Spinner text="Loading published data…" />}
      {!loading && error && isPending && (
        <div role="status" aria-live="polite" className="notice">
          <strong>{title}</strong>
          <p>{error}</p>
          <p>Choose an earlier completed session, or check again once this session has finished and its data is published.</p>
          {retryAt !== null && retryAt > now && (
            <p>You can try again after {new Date(retryAt).toLocaleString([], {
              month: "short", day: "numeric", hour: "numeric", minute: "2-digit", second: "2-digit",
            })} (your local time). Data may still be pending then.</p>
          )}
          {retry && (
            <button className="btn btn--secondary" type="button"
              disabled={retryAt !== null && retryAt > now} onClick={retry}>
              Check again
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      {!loading && error && !isPending && (
        <p role="alert" className="notice notice--error">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

import {
  ResponsiveContainer,
  LineChart,
  AreaChart,
  Line,
  Area,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  Tooltip,
  CartesianGrid,
  ReferenceLine,
} from 'recharts'

interface Props {
  title: string
  data: Record<string, number | null>[]
  dataKey1: string
  dataKey2?: string
  color1?: string
  color2?: string
  isArea?: boolean
}

export default function TelemetryChart({
  title,
  data,
  dataKey1,
  dataKey2,
  color1 = 'var(--f1-red)',
  color2 = 'var(--team-mercedes)',
  isArea = false,
}: Props) {
  const Chart = isArea ? AreaChart : LineChart

  return (
    <div className="chart-card">
      <div className="chart-card__title">{title}</div>
      <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={220}>
        <Chart data={data} margin={{ top: 4, right: 8, bottom: 0, left: -16 }}>
          <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#2d2d3f" />
          <XAxis
            dataKey="distance"
            tick={{ fill: '#949aab', fontSize: 10 }}
            tickLine={false}
            axisLine={{ stroke: '#2d2d3f' }}
          />
          <YAxis
            tick={{ fill: '#949aab', fontSize: 10 }}
            tickLine={false}
            axisLine={{ stroke: '#2d2d3f' }}
          />
          <Tooltip
            contentStyle={{
              background: '#1e1e2e',
              border: '1px solid #38384d',
              borderRadius: 4,
              fontSize: 12,
            }}
          />

          {isArea ? (
            <>
              <ReferenceLine y={0} stroke="#555" />
              <Area
                type="monotone"
                dataKey={dataKey1}
                stroke={color1}
                fill={color1}
                fillOpacity={0.2}
                dot={false}
                isAnimationActive={false}
              />
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Line
                type="monotone"
                dataKey={dataKey1}
                stroke={color1}
                dot={false}
                strokeWidth={2}
                isAnimationActive={false}
              />
              {dataKey2 && (
                <Line
                  type="monotone"
                  dataKey={dataKey2}
                  stroke={color2}
                  dot={false}
                  strokeWidth={2}
                  isAnimationActive={false}
                />
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </Chart>
      </ResponsiveContainer>
    </div>
  )
}

import { useState } from "react";
import type { Standing } from "../api";
import CarGlyph, { teamColor } from "./CarGlyph";

export default function StandingsCircuit({
  rows,
  previousRound,
  round,
}: {
  rows: Standing[];
  previousRound: number | null;
  round: number | null;
}) {
  const [current, setCurrent] = useState(true);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState("");
  const focus = rows.find((r) => r.id === selected);
  return (
    <section className="visual-panel">
      <div className="section-heading">
        <div>
          <h2>Championship on track</h2>
          <p className="text-gray">
            Schematic rank positions · spacing does not represent seconds or
            points
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          className="btn btn--secondary"
          disabled={!previousRound}
          onClick={() => setCurrent((v) => !v)}
        >
          {current
            ? `Replay change from round ${previousRound ?? "—"}`
            : `Show round ${round}`}
        </button>
      </div>
      <svg
        className="standings-circuit"
        viewBox="0 0 1000 550"
        role="img"
        aria-label={`Championship positions after round ${current ? round : previousRound}`}
      >
        <ellipse
          cx="500"
          cy="260"
          rx="405"
          ry="185"
          fill="none"
          stroke="#39394d"
          strokeWidth="68"
        />
        <ellipse
          cx="500"
          cy="260"
          rx="405"
          ry="185"
          fill="none"
          stroke="#77778a"
          strokeDasharray="10 16"
        />
        <text
          x="500"
          y="240"
          textAnchor="middle"
          fill="#fff"
          fontSize="30"
          fontWeight="900"
        >
          ROUND {current ? round : previousRound}
        </text>
        <text x="500" y="275" textAnchor="middle" fill="#aaaabe" fontSize="16">
          {current ? "Current order" : "Previous published order"}
        </text>
        {rows.map((row) => {
          const pos = current ? row.position : row.previousPosition;
          if (pos == null) return null;
          const angle =
            -Math.PI / 2 + ((pos - 1) * 2 * Math.PI) / Math.max(rows.length, 1);
          const x = 500 + 405 * Math.cos(angle),
            y = 260 + 185 * Math.sin(angle);
          return (
            <g
              key={row.id}
              className="rank-car"
              style={{ transform: `translate(${x}px, ${y}px)` }}
              role="button"
              tabIndex={0}
              aria-label={`${row.name}, position ${pos}`}
              onClick={() => setSelected(row.id)}
              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") setSelected(row.id);
              }}
            >
              <title>
                {row.name} · P{pos} ·{" "}
                {current ? row.points : row.previousPoints} points
              </title>
              <CarGlyph
                color={teamColor(row.team)}
                label={
                  row.label.length > 10 ? row.label.slice(0, 10) : row.label
                }
              />
              <text x="22" y="4" fontSize="13" fill="#fff">
                {pos}
              </text>
            </g>
          );
        })}
      </svg>
      {focus && (
        <p className="notice">
          {focus.name} · {focus.points} points ·{" "}
          {focus.positionChange == null
            ? "No previous snapshot"
            : `${focus.positionChange > 0 ? "+" : ""}${focus.positionChange} places`}{" "}
          · {focus.pointsChange ?? "—"} points since last round
        </p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

FILE: ./frontend/src/hooks/useSeasonAnalysis.ts
============================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { request } from "../api";
interface Job<T> {
  id: string;
  status: string;
  completed: number;
  total: number;
  data: T | null;
  error: string | null;
}
export function useSeasonAnalysis<T>(era: string) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<T | null>(null),
    [loading, setLoading] = useState(false),
    [error, setError] = useState(""),
    [progress, setProgress] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
    setData(null);
    setError("");
    setLoading(true);
    setProgress("Queued");
    const update = (job: Job<T>) => {
      if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
      setProgress(`${job.completed} / ${job.total || "?"} rounds`);
      if (job.status === "complete") {
        setData(job.data);
        setLoading(false);
      } else if (job.status === "failed") {
        setError(job.error ?? "Analysis failed");
        setLoading(false);
      } else
        timer = setTimeout(() => {
          request<Job<T>>(`/best-car/season-jobs/status/${job.id}`, {
            signal: controller.signal,
          })
            .then(update)
            .catch(fail);
        }, 1500);
    };
    const fail = (e: unknown) => {
      if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
        setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Analysis failed");
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };
    request<Job<T>>(`/best-car/season-jobs/${era}`, {
      method: "POST",
      signal: controller.signal,
    })
      .then(update)
      .catch(fail);
    return () => {
      controller.abort();
      if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [era]);
  return { data, loading, error, progress };
}


============================================================
FILE: ./frontend/src/hooks/useApi.ts
============================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { ApiError, request } from "../api";

/** Abort obsolete requests, clear stale results, and expose every failure. */
export function useApi<T>(path: string | null, refresh = 0) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<T | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [apiError, setApiError] = useState<ApiError | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    setData(null);
    setError("");
    setApiError(null);
    setLoading(Boolean(path));
    if (!path) return () => controller.abort();
    request<T>(path, { signal: controller.signal })
      .then((value) => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setData(value);
      })
      .catch((e: unknown) => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unable to load data");
          setApiError(e instanceof ApiError ? e : null);
        }
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false);
      });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [path, refresh, attempt]);
  return { data, error, apiError, loading, retry: () => setAttempt((v) => v + 1) };
}

import { Navigate, Routes, Route } from "react-router-dom";
import Layout from "./components/Layout";
import HomePage from "./pages/HomePage";
import PredictionPage from "./pages/PredictionPage";
import GhostCarPage from "./pages/GhostCarPage";
import BestCarPage from "./pages/BestCarPage";
import WeekendPage from "./pages/WeekendPage";
import MotorsportPage from "./pages/MotorsportPage";
export default function App() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/" element={<Layout />}>
        <Route index element={<HomePage />} />
        <Route path="weekend" element={<WeekendPage />} />
        <Route path="prediction" element={<PredictionPage />} />
        <Route path="ghost-car" element={<GhostCarPage />} />
        <Route path="looking-back" element={<BestCarPage />} />
        <Route
          path="best-car"
          element={<Navigate to="/looking-back" replace />}
        />
        <Route path="not-just-f1" element={<MotorsportPage />} />
        <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
      </Route>
    </Routes>
  );
}


============================================================
FILE: ./frontend/src/api.ts
============================================================
const BASE = "/api";

/** Preserve provider status so expected publication delays get a helpful UI. */
export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly retryAt: number | null;

  constructor(
    message: string,
    readonly status: number,
    readonly code: string | null = null,
    retryAfter: string | null = null,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
    const value = retryAfter?.trim();
    const retryAt = value
      ? /^\d+$/.test(value)
        ? Date.now() + Number(value) * 1000
        : Date.parse(value)
      : NaN;
    this.retryAt = Number.isFinite(retryAt) ? retryAt : null;
  }
}

export async function request<T>(
  url: string,
  options?: RequestInit,
): Promise<T> {
  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${url}`, {
    ...options,
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...options?.headers },
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({ detail: res.statusText }));
    throw new ApiError(
      typeof body?.detail === "string" ? body.detail : `Request failed: ${res.status}`,
      res.status,
      res.headers?.get("X-F1-Data-Error") ?? null,
      res.headers?.get("Retry-After") ?? null,
    );
  }
  return res.json();
}

/* ── Common ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

export const fetchSeasons = () => request<{ seasons: number[] }>("/seasons");

export const fetchSchedule = (year: number) =>
  request<{ year: number; events: EventInfo[] }>(`/schedule/${year}`);

export const fetchSessions = (year: number, round: number) =>
  request<{ sessions: SessionInfo[] }>(`/sessions/${year}/${round}`);

export const fetchDrivers = (year: number, round: number, session: string) =>
  request<{ drivers: DriverInfo[] }>(`/drivers/${year}/${round}/${session}`);

/* ── Prediction ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

export const fetchModels = () =>
  request<{ models: string[] }>("/prediction/models");

export interface PredictionResult {
  position: number;
  driver: string;
  winProbability: number;
}

export interface PredictionResponse {
  year: number;
  gp: string;
  session: string;
  sessionLabel: string;
  model: string;
  monteCarloRuns: number;
  predictions: PredictionResult[];
  runId: string;
  mode: string;
  createdAt: string;
  warnings: string[];
  winningMargins: Record<string, number> | null;
}

export const runPrediction = (body: {
  year: number;
  gp: string;
  session: string;
  model: string;
}) =>
  request<PredictionResponse>("/prediction/run", {
    method: "POST",
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });

export const fetchGrandPrix = (year: number) =>
  request<{ year: number; events: EventInfo[] }>(
    `/prediction/grand-prix/${year}`,
  );

export interface TelemetryData {
  x: number[];
  y: number[];
  speed: number[];
  throttle: number[];
  brake: number[];
  gear: number[];
  distance: number[];
  time: number[];
  drs: (number | null)[];
}

export interface LapData {
  driver?: string;
  abbreviation?: string;
  team: string;
  lapTime: string;
  lapTimeSeconds: number;
  compound: string;
  telemetry: TelemetryData;
}

export interface ComparisonResponse {
  eventName: string;
  dataSource?: string;
  warnings?: string[];
  driver1: LapData;
  driver2: LapData;
  comparison: {
    distance: number[];
    speed1: number[];
    speed2: number[];
    speedDelta: number[];
    timeDelta: number[];
    throttle1: number[];
    throttle2: number[];
    brake1: number[];
    brake2: number[];
    gear1: number[];
    gear2: number[];
    drs1: (number | null)[];
    drs2: (number | null)[];
  };
}

export const fetchTrackLayout = (
  year: number,
  round: number,

) =>

  year: number,
  round: number,
  session: string,
  driver: string,
) =>

    `/ghost-car/fastest-lap/${year}/${round}/${session}/${driver}`,
  );

  year: number,
  round: number,
  session: string,

  d2: string,

    `/ghost-car/compare/${year}/${round}/${session}/${d1}/${d2}`,
  );

      medianLapTime: number;
      fastestLap: number;
      laps: number;
    }[];
  }>(`/best-car/race-pace/${year}/${encodeURIComponent(race)}`);

}
export interface TeamComparison {
  team: string;
  first: string;
  second: string;
  qualifying: {
    segment: string | null;
    deltaSeconds: number | null;
    reason: string;
  };
  raceDeltaSeconds: number | null;
  matchedLaps: number;
  interval95: number[] | null;
  paceReversal: boolean;
  samples: {
    lap: number;
    first: number;
    second: number;
    delta: number;
    compound: string;
    ageA: number;
    ageB: number;
  }[];
  tyreTrends: Record<string, TyreTrend[]>;
}
export interface FieldPace {
  driver: string;
  team: string;
  qualifyingPosition: number | null;
  finishPosition: number | null;
  matchedLaps: number;
  relativePacePercent: number | null;
}
export interface WeekendResponse {
  dataSources?: Record<string, string>;
  fieldPace?: FieldPace[];
  year: number;
  round: number;
  eventName: string;
  teams: TeamComparison[];
  weather: Record<string, number | boolean | null> | null;
  warnings: string[];
  limitations: string[];
  method: string;
  unavailable: string[];
}
export interface Score {
  events: number;
  brier: number;
  logLoss: number;
  top1Accuracy: number;
}
export interface ModelInfo {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  improvements: string[];
  limitations: string[];
  sessions: string[];
  metrics: Score | null;
  trainedThrough: string | null;
  trainingEvents?: number;
  baselines?: Record<string, Score>;
  improvesGridLogLoss?: boolean;
  previousComparable?: Score | null;
}
export interface ModelCatalog {
  models: string[];
  catalog: ModelInfo[];
  defaultModel: string;
  trainingCommand: string;
}
export interface Evaluation {
  status: string;
  detail?: string;
  mode?: string;
  winner?: string;
  predictedWinner?: string;
  winnerProbability?: number;
  correct?: boolean;
  brier?: number;
  logLoss?: number;
  note?: string;
  asOf?: string;
  results?: {
    position: number;
    driver: string;
    predictedProbability: number;
  }[];
}
export interface RunSummary {
  runId: string;
  year: number;
  gp: string;
  model: string;
  mode: string;
  createdAt: string;
}
export interface EngineeringTopic {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  documented: string;
  measure: string;
  cannotInfer: string;
  sourceLabel: string;
  sourceUrl: string;
}
